     * stored: the passkey credential id (not secret) and sealed RAILGUN
     * checkpoints are all that touch disk.
     */
    import { SEPOLIA } from "@prawl/anon-rpc";
    import { createPasskey, deriveWalletKeys, unlockWithPasskey, type WalletKeys } from "@prawl/keys";
    import type { Balance, Hex } from "@prawl/railgun";
    import { generatePrivateKey, privateKeyToAddress } from "viem/accounts";
    import { keccak256, toHex } from "viem";
    import { anon } from "./anon";
    import { store } from "./store";
    import { ActivityLog, type Activity, type ActivityKind } from "./activity";
    import { railgun } from "../railgun/controller";
    import { publicBalance, sendCalls, sendUserOp, UnconfirmedError, type SentTx } from "./tx";
    import { activateCall, coinStatus, commitCall, depositCall, erc20Balance, loadProblems, privateLoadProblems, redeemCall, redeemOutcome, redeemToShieldCall, refreshCall, revertReason, simulateRedeem } from "../offline/escrow";
    import { OFFLINE_CHAIN_ID, payoutValue, type LoadPayload, type LoadPrivatePayload, type RedeemPayload, type RedeemShieldPayload, type RefreshPayload, type ShieldRequest } from "../offline/payload";
    import { shieldRequestFromCall } from "../offline/shield";
    import { tellPhone } from "../offline/bridge";
    
    /** ERC-4337 bundler (the RAILGUN paymaster pays gas from the private balance). */
    export const BUNDLER_URL = "https://public.pimlico.io/v2/11155111/rpc";
    
    export type WalletPhase = "locked" | "unlocking" | "opening" | "syncing" | "ready" | "error";
    
    export interface WalletState {
      phase: WalletPhase;
      hasPasskey: boolean;
      chainId: number;
      railgunAddress?: string;
      /** The wallet's public EVM account: the way in and out, never the main balance. */
      evmAddress?: string;
      syncedBlock?: bigint;
      head?: bigint;
      deploymentBlock?: bigint;
      balances?: Balance[];
      wrappedBaseToken?: string;
      /** RAILGUN smart wallet of this chain (target of shield calls). */
      smartWallet?: string;
      restored?: boolean;
      /** True when this open scans RAILGUN history from the start (no checkpoint, no usable snapshot). */
      fullScan?: boolean;
      /** A restored wallet's scan restarts here (its birth, bounded) on top of the snapshot. */
      replayFrom?: bigint;
      /** Block of the public pool snapshot a new or restored wallet started from. */
      snapshotBlock?: bigint;
      lastSyncMs?: number;
      /** Our operations whose PPOI proofs are still queued (submitted on each sync). */
      poiQueue?: number;
      /** This wallet's own history, newest first (sealed on disk). */
      activity?: Activity[];
      /** RAILGUN unshield fee of this chain, basis points (from the chain config). */
      unshieldFeeBps?: number;
      /** Gas the paymaster took from the private balance for this wallet's last private operation. */
      privateGasWei?: bigint;
      /** Last PPOI submission problem, if any. The proofs stay queued. */
      poiError?: string;
      /** Public ETH on the EVM account (the way in and out). */
      publicWei?: bigint;
      /** Progress or result of the current shield/unshield/send. */
      action?: { kind: string; status: string; done?: boolean; error?: string; txs?: SentTx[] };
      error?: string;
    }
    
    const CREDENTIAL_KEY = "passkey:credential-id";
    
    let keys: WalletKeys | undefined;
    let activityLog: ActivityLog | undefined;
    /**
     * Bumped whenever the unlocked wallet changes (unlock, lock, rescan). Work that
     * started under an earlier epoch (a sync, a private send, a history write)
     * must not touch the screen or another wallet's history after the change.
     */
    let epoch = 0;
    
    /** Set the action result only if the wallet it belongs to is still the open one. */
    function updateAction(forEpoch: number, action: NonNullable<WalletState["action"]>): void {
      if (forEpoch === epoch) update({ action });
    }
    
    /** The action result for a thrown error: "status unknown" when the submission may still land, else "failed". */
    function failedAction(failure: string, e: unknown, message: string = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)): NonNullable<WalletState["action"]> {
      if (e instanceof UnconfirmedError) {
        return {
          kind: "",
          status: "Status unknown",
          done: true,
          error: `${e.message}. Do not repeat it yet: after the next refresh check your balance and Activity, since a retry could pay twice.`,
        };
      }
      return { kind: "", status: failure, done: true, error: message };
    }
    
    /** Record finished transactions in the sealed history. Never fails the action. */
    async function remember(log: ActivityLog | undefined, forEpoch: number, kind: ActivityKind, amountWei: bigint, to: string | undefined, txs: readonly SentTx[]): Promise<void> {
      try {
        for (const t of txs) {
          // `log` is the history of the wallet that started the action, never whichever is open now.
          const list = await log?.add({ at: Date.now(), kind, amount: amountWei.toString(), ...(to ? { to } : {}), txHash: t.hash, ok: t.status === "success" });
          if (list && forEpoch === epoch) update({ activity: list });
        }
      } catch {
        // history is a convenience; the transaction already happened
      }
    }

    const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
    
    function update(patch: Partial<WalletState>): void {
      state = { ...state, ...patch };
      for (const l of listeners) l();
    }
    
    export const walletState = {
      get: (): WalletState => state,
      subscribe(fn: () => void): () => void {
        listeners.add(fn);
        return () => listeners.delete(fn);
      },
    };
    
    void store.get<string>(CREDENTIAL_KEY).then((id) => update({ hasPasskey: !!id }));
    
    /**
     * How far this device's clock runs ahead of the chain, measured once from the
     * latest block (best effort; 0 until known or when the clock is not ahead). The
     * passkey stores the wallet's creation time, and a fast clock would store a
     * time later than the real one.
     */
    let clockSkewS = 0;
    async function measureClockSkew(): Promise<void> {
      try {
        const block = (await anon.read(anon.identities.publicScope(`clock:${SEPOLIA}`), SEPOLIA, "eth_getBlockByNumber", ["latest", false])) as { timestamp: string };
        const skew = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - Number(BigInt(block.timestamp));
        // A block is up to a slot and a bit of network delay old: only a clear lead counts.
        if (skew > 120) clockSkewS = skew;
      } catch {
        // unknown: the restore's 24 h slack still covers an ordinary error
      }
    }
    void measureClockSkew();
    
    railgun().onProgress((p) => update({ syncedBlock: BigInt(p.synced), head: BigInt(p.head) }));
    
    interface StartOptions {
      fresh: boolean;
      bornAt?: number;
      discardCheckpoint?: boolean;
    }
    async function start(root: Uint8Array, opts: StartOptions): Promise<void> {
      epoch += 1;
      keys = await deriveWalletKeys(root);
      root.fill(0);
      await openRailgun(opts);
    }
    
    async function openRailgun(opts: StartOptions): Promise<void> {
      if (!keys) throw new Error("wallet is locked");
      const evmAddress = privateKeyToAddress(keys.evmAccount);
      update({ phase: "opening", evmAddress, error: undefined });
      const rg = railgun();
      const { deployment, rpcUrl } = await rg.prepare(state.chainId, evmAddress);
      // Record name derived from the account, so two wallets on one device never share a checkpoint.
      const walletTag = `${state.chainId}:${keccak256(toHex(evmAddress)).slice(2, 18)}`;
      const checkpointName = `railgun:${walletTag}`;
      activityLog = new ActivityLog(store, keys.storage, `activity:${walletTag}`);
      void activityLog.load().then((activity) => update({ activity }), () => update({ activity: [] }));
      const opened = await rg.call("open", {
        chainId: state.chainId,
        rpcUrl,
        spendingKey: keys.railgunSpending,
        viewingKey: keys.railgunViewing,
        storageKey: keys.storage,
        checkpointName,
        fresh: opts.fresh,
        ...(opts.bornAt !== undefined ? { bornAt: opts.bornAt } : {}),
        ...(opts.discardCheckpoint ? { discardCheckpoint: true } : {}),
      });
      update({
        railgunAddress: opened.address,
        syncedBlock: BigInt(opened.syncedBlock),
        deploymentBlock: BigInt(deployment.deploymentBlock),
        wrappedBaseToken: deployment.wrappedBaseToken.toLowerCase(),
        unshieldFeeBps: deployment.unshieldFeeBps,
        smartWallet: deployment.smartWallet,
        restored: opened.restored,
        fullScan: opened.fullScan,
        snapshotBlock: opened.snapshotBlock ? BigInt(opened.snapshotBlock) : undefined,
        replayFrom: opened.replayFrom ? BigInt(opened.replayFrom) : undefined,
      });
      await sync();
      startAutoSync();
    }
    
    /** One sync at a time: a second caller (button, timer, after an action) waits for the running one. */
    let running: Promise<void> | undefined;
    export function sync(): Promise<void> {
      running ??= doSync().finally(() => (running = undefined));
      return running;
    }
    
    async function doSync(): Promise<void> {
      if (!keys) return;
      const rg = railgun();
      const myEpoch = epoch;
      update({ phase: "syncing", error: undefined });
      try {
        const t0 = performance.now();
        const head = await anon.head(state.chainId);
        update({ head });
        await rg.call("sync", { head: head.toString() });
        const [balances, publicWei] = await Promise.all([
          rg.call("balances", undefined as never),
          state.evmAddress ? publicBalance(anon, state.chainId, state.evmAddress).catch(() => state.publicWei) : undefined,
        ]);
        if (myEpoch !== epoch) return; // locked or switched while we waited: show nothing of this wallet
        update({ phase: "ready", balances, lastSyncMs: performance.now() - t0, ...(publicWei !== undefined ? { publicWei } : {}) });
      } catch (e) {
        if (myEpoch === epoch) update({ phase: "error", error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) });
        return;
      }
      await submitPendingPoi(myEpoch);
    }
    
    /**
     * After our private send or unshield lands, its PPOI proofs must reach the
     * PPOI node, or the recipient's notes never become spendable. The queue lives
     * in the checkpoint; a proof the node cannot take yet (transaction not
     * indexed, input not valid yet) stays queued for the next sync. Failures here
    /**
     * Deploys the full Veil batch-swap stack to Sepolia from Node (no MetaMask):
     *   vUSD, vETH (demo ERC20s) → V4 pool (1:1, seeded liquidity)
     *   → c-vUSD, c-vETH (ERC-7984 wrappers) → VeilBatchSwap
     *
     * Keys come from protocol/.env (PROTOCOL_PRIVATE_KEY). Run:
     *   cd protocol && node scripts/deploy-stack.mjs
     */
    
    import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, existsSync, mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';
    import { resolve, dirname } from 'node:path';
    import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
    import { ethers } from 'ethers';
    
    const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
    const root = resolve(here, '../..');
    
    function loadEnvFile(path) {
      try {
        for (const line of readFileSync(path, 'utf8').split(/\r?\n/)) {
          const m = line.match(/^\s*([A-Z0-9_]+)\s*=\s*(.*?)\s*$/);
          if (m && process.env[m[1]] === undefined) process.env[m[1]] = m[2].replace(/^['"]|['"]$/g, '');
        }
      } catch {}
    }
    loadEnvFile(resolve(root, '.env.local'));
    loadEnvFile(resolve(root, 'protocol/.env'));
    
    const RPC = process.env.RPC_URL_SEPOLIA || process.env.SEPOLIA_RPC_URL;
    if (!RPC) throw new Error('Set SEPOLIA_RPC_URL in .env.local');
    if (!process.env.PROTOCOL_PRIVATE_KEY) throw new Error('Set PROTOCOL_PRIVATE_KEY in protocol/.env');
    
    const SEPOLIA = {
      chainId: 11155111,
      poolManager: '0xE03A1074c86CFeDd5C142C4F04F1a1536e203543',
      universalRouter: '0x3a9d48ab9751398bbfa63ad67599bb04e4bdf98b',
      permit2: '0x000000000022D473030F116dDEE9F6B43aC78BA3',
      poolModifyLiquidityTest: '0x0c478023803a644c94c4ce1c1e7b9a087e411b0a',
    };
    
    const provider = new ethers.JsonRpcProvider(RPC, 11155111, { staticNetwork: true });
    const keeper = new ethers.Wallet(process.env.PROTOCOL_PRIVATE_KEY, provider);
    const log = (...args) => console.log(`[${new Date().toISOString().slice(11, 19)}]`, ...args);
    
    async function wait(hash, label) {
      log(`${label}: ${hash}`);
      const receipt = await provider.waitForTransaction(hash, 1, 180_000);
      if (receipt.status !== 1) throw new Error(`${label} reverted: ${hash}`);
      log(`${label}: mined block ${receipt.blockNumber} gas=${receipt.gasUsed}`);
      return receipt;
    }
    
    async function deployArtifact(artifactPath, args, label) {
      const { abi, bytecode } = JSON.parse(readFileSync(artifactPath, 'utf8'));
      const factory = new ethers.ContractFactory(abi, bytecode, keeper);
      const contract = await factory.deploy(...args);
      await contract.waitForDeployment();
      const deploymentReceipt = await contract.deploymentTransaction().wait();
      const address = await contract.getAddress();
      log(`${label}: ${address}`);
      return { address, abi, deployedBlock: deploymentReceipt.blockNumber };
    }
    
    // ---- PoolKey ABI encoding (v4-core order: currency0, currency1, fee, tickSpacing, hooks) ----
    const addr = (a) => ethers.zeroPadValue(a.toLowerCase(), 32);
    const poolKeyEncoding = (pk) => addr(pk.currency0) + addr(pk.currency1)
      + ethers.zeroPadValue(ethers.toBeHex(pk.fee), 32).slice(2)
      + ethers.zeroPadValue(ethers.toBeHex(BigInt.asIntN(24, pk.tickSpacing)), 32).slice(2)
      + addr(pk.hooks);
    
    const STATE_PATH = resolve(here, '../deployments/state.json');
    mkdirSync(resolve(here, '../deployments'), { recursive: true });
    const state = existsSync(STATE_PATH) ? JSON.parse(readFileSync(STATE_PATH, 'utf8')) : {};
    const saveState = () => writeFileSync(STATE_PATH, JSON.stringify(state, null, 2));
    
    async function main() {
      const balance = await provider.getBalance(keeper.address);
      log(`keeper ${keeper.address} balance ${ethers.formatEther(balance)} ETH`);
      if (balance < ethers.parseEther('0.02')) throw new Error('Keeper needs ≥0.02 Sepolia ETH — fund it from a faucet or your MetaMask.');
    
      const minRoundSeconds = Number(process.env.VEIL_MIN_ROUND_SECONDS || 60);
      const minUsers = Number(process.env.VEIL_MIN_USERS || 2);
    
      // 1. Demo tokens (MockERC20 — open mint, from the main project's artifact)
      const mockBin = readFileSync(resolve(root, 'public/artifacts/MockERC20.bin'), 'utf8').trim().replace(/^0x/i, '');
      const mockAbi = JSON.parse(readFileSync(resolve(root, 'public/artifacts/MockERC20.abi'), 'utf8'));
      const deploySimple = async (name, symbol) => {
        const factory = new ethers.ContractFactory(mockAbi, `0x${mockBin}`, keeper);
        const c = await factory.deploy(name, symbol);
        await c.waitForDeployment();
        return { address: await c.getAddress(), abi: mockAbi };
      };
      let vusd, veth;
      if (state.tokens) {
        vusd = { address: state.tokens.vusd, abi: mockAbi };
        veth = { address: state.tokens.veth, abi: mockAbi };
        log(`resuming with vUSD ${vusd.address} · vETH ${veth.address}`);
      } else {
        vusd = await deploySimple('Veil Demo Dollar', 'vUSD');
        veth = await deploySimple('Veil Demo Ether', 'vETH');
        state.tokens = { vusd: vusd.address, veth: veth.address };
        saveState();
        log(`vUSD ${vusd.address} · vETH ${veth.address}`);
      }
    
      if (!state.minted) {
        for (const token of [vusd, veth]) {
          const c = new ethers.Contract(token.address, mockAbi, keeper);
          await wait((await c.mint(keeper.address, 1_000_000n * 10n ** 18n)).hash, `mint 1M ${token === vusd ? 'vUSD' : 'vETH'}`);
        }
        state.minted = true;
        saveState();
      }
    
      // 2. Uniswap v4 pool: initialize at 1:1, seed liquidity
      const [currency0, currency1] = [vusd.address, veth.address]
        .map(a => a.toLowerCase())
        .sort((a, b) => (BigInt(a) < BigInt(b) ? -1 : 1));
      const poolKey = { currency0, currency1, fee: 3000, tickSpacing: 60, hooks: ethers.ZeroAddress };
    
      const manager = new ethers.Contract(SEPOLIA.poolManager, [
        'function initialize((address,address,uint24,int24,address) calldata key, uint160 sqrtPriceX96) external returns (int24)',
      ], keeper);
      // PoolKey struct encoding for ethers: pass as array in declaration order.
      if (!state.poolInitialized) {
        await wait((await manager.initialize(
          [poolKey.currency0, poolKey.currency1, poolKey.fee, poolKey.tickSpacing, poolKey.hooks],
          1n << 96n,
        )).hash, 'initialize V4 pool');
        state.poolInitialized = true;
        saveState();
      }
    
      // poolModifyLiquidityTest helper: modifyLiquidity(PoolKey,(int24,int24,int256,bytes),bytes)
      const helper = new ethers.Contract(SEPOLIA.poolModifyLiquidityTest, [
        'function modifyLiquidity((address,address,uint24,int24,address) calldata key, (int24,int24,int256,bytes32) calldata params, bytes calldata hookData) external returns (int256)',
      ], keeper);
      if (!state.liquidityAdded) {
        for (const token of [vusd, veth]) {
          const c = new ethers.Contract(token.address, mockAbi, keeper);
          await wait((await c.approve(SEPOLIA.poolModifyLiquidityTest, 2n ** 256n - 1n)).hash, `approve ${token === vusd ? 'vUSD' : 'vETH'} for liquidity helper`);
        }
        await wait((await helper.modifyLiquidity(
          [poolKey.currency0, poolKey.currency1, poolKey.fee, poolKey.tickSpacing, poolKey.hooks],
          [-600, 600, 10_000n * 10n ** 18n, '0x' + '0'.repeat(64)], // salt = bytes32(0)
          '0x',
        )).hash, 'add 1:1 liquidity');
        state.liquidityAdded = true;
        saveState();
      }
    
      // 3. Confidential wrappers + batcher
      const artDir = resolve(here, '../artifacts/contracts');
      let wrapper, wrapperB, batch;
      let batchDeployedBlock;
      const redeployBatch = process.env.VEIL_REDEPLOY_BATCH === '1';
      if (state.stack) {
        ({ cTokenA: wrapper, cTokenB: wrapperB } = state.stack);
        batchDeployedBlock = state.stack.deployedBlock ?? null;
        wrapper = { address: wrapper };
        wrapperB = { address: wrapperB };
        if (!redeployBatch) {
          batch = { address: state.stack.batch };
          log(`resuming with wrappers ${wrapper.address} / ${wrapperB.address} and batch ${batch.address}`);
        } else {
          log(`reusing wrappers ${wrapper.address} / ${wrapperB.address}; deploying one new batcher`);
        }
      } else {
        wrapper = await deployArtifact(resolve(artDir, 'VeilConfidentialWrapper.sol/VeilConfidentialWrapper.json'), [vusd.address], 'c-vUSD wrapper');
        wrapperB = await deployArtifact(resolve(artDir, 'VeilConfidentialWrapper.sol/VeilConfidentialWrapper.json'), [veth.address], 'c-vETH wrapper');
      }
      if (!state.stack || redeployBatch) {
        batch = await deployArtifact(resolve(artDir, 'VeilBatchSwap.sol/VeilBatchSwap.json'), [
          SEPOLIA.universalRouter,
          SEPOLIA.permit2,
          wrapper.address,
          wrapperB.address,
          [poolKey.currency0, poolKey.currency1, poolKey.fee, poolKey.tickSpacing, poolKey.hooks],
          minRoundSeconds,
          minUsers,
          keeper.address,
        ], redeployBatch ? 'VeilBatchSwap (redeploy)' : 'VeilBatchSwap');
        batchDeployedBlock = batch.deployedBlock;
        state.stack = { cTokenA: wrapper.address, cTokenB: wrapperB.address, batch: batch.address, deployedBlock: batchDeployedBlock };
        saveState();
      }
    
      const config = {
        chainId: 11155111,
        deployed: true,
        protocolVersion: 2,
        batch: batch.address,
        cTokenA: wrapper.address,
        cTokenB: wrapperB.address,
        tokenA: vusd.address,
        tokenB: veth.address,
        poolKey,
        deployedBy: keeper.address,
        settler: keeper.address,
        deployedBlock: batchDeployedBlock,
        deployedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        minRoundSeconds,
        minUsers,
      };
    
      mkdirSync(resolve(here, '../deployments'), { recursive: true });
      writeFileSync(resolve(here, '../deployments/sepolia.json'), JSON.stringify(config, null, 2));
      writeFileSync(resolve(root, 'batch-config.json'), JSON.stringify(config, null, 2));
      log('Deployment saved → protocol/deployments/sepolia.json + batch-config.json');
      log(JSON.stringify(config, null, 2));
    }
    
    main().catch((error) => {
      console.error('DEPLOY FAILED:', error.message);
      process.exit(1);
    });
    ✓ • 0ms

## Assistant

The plan should target a **working, production-ready private swap in Prawl**, with a Swap button opening a dashboard and one Uniswap pool for WETH and a custom ERC-20. The ETHGlobal project provides a useful encrypted batching mechanism, but reaching production requires more than porting its demo: in particular, its 1:1 matching, public funding and exit boundaries, operator dependency, and small-batch privacy limits need explicit solutions.

The existing Swap dashboard is a disabled starting point. It must become a real transaction interface backed by deployed, verified contracts and Prawl’s wallet infrastructure.

## Target user experience

From the wallet home screen, the user selects **Swap** and sees:

- WETH and custom-token balances, clearly distinguishing RAILGUN, public-account and confidential swap-vault balances.
- A direction selector and amount input.
- An indicative price, pool and network fees, minimum output or execution protection, and estimated settlement timing.
- A clear explanation of any funding or exit step that creates a public transaction.
- The current batch status and the user’s order status.
- Actions to fund the swap vault, submit an encrypted order, view the resulting confidential balance, and withdraw or return supported assets to RAILGUN.
- Recovery information when a transaction is pending, a round is delayed, or a network request has an unknown outcome.

Existing wallet functionality—passkeys, private transfers, shielding, withdrawals, Tor networking, offline cash, activity and recovery—must continue to work.

## Phase 1: Specify the privacy and product requirements

Before implementing contracts, define exactly what the feature promises and what an observer can see.

1. Trace Prawl’s current WETH lifecycle from public ETH to RAILGUN and back, including token support, fees, one-time accounts, transaction signing and PPOI.
2. Trace the ETHGlobal mechanism end to end: confidential wrappers, encrypted deposits, orders, matching, decryption, Uniswap settlement, crediting and exits.
3. Write a threat model for users, keepers, RPC providers, FHE infrastructure, pool traders and observers.
4. Define the intended privacy properties for:
   - Order direction, amount and output.
   - Wallet identity and participation in a batch.
   - Funding, withdrawal and movement between RAILGUN and the swap vault.
   - Network metadata and gateway requests.
   - Small batches and aggregate net trades.
5. Specify acceptable behavior if encryption, decryption, settlement, keeper availability or network access fails.

**Decision gate:** The current prototype exposes orderer addresses, funding and exits, and the aggregate Uniswap trade. If the product requires those to be hidden as well, the protocol needs additional mechanisms—not just integration work. The implementation must not claim stronger privacy than the design can demonstrate.

## Phase 2: Choose a sound pricing and settlement design

The prototype matches opposite orders at 1:1 and applies a fixed 90% output floor. That is not suitable for an ordinary WETH/custom-token market.

1. Define the custom token’s purpose, supply, minting permissions, decimals and supported network.
2. Choose the pool’s initial price, fee tier, liquidity range and liquidity provider arrangement.
3. Replace fixed-parity matching with **price-aware accounting**, including a defensible price source, normalization across token decimals and rounding rules.
4. Define execution protection for both internally matched and Uniswap-settled portions:
   - User-specified limits or an equivalent enforceable protection.
   - Maximum price deviation and stale-price handling.
   - Deadline and cancellation/refund behavior.
5. Define how fees, rounding dust and partial fills are accounted for.
6. Ensure settlement remains solvent even with adversarial order combinations, pool price movements and failed swaps.

**Decision gate:** An indicative quote is not enough. The contract must enforce the agreed execution protection without revealing private order details beyond the documented privacy model.

## Phase 3: Build the confidential swap contracts

Implement or adapt the mechanism into a maintainable protocol package owned by Prawl.

### Confidential custody and orders

- Support the chosen WETH and custom-token wrappers.
- Track encrypted balances and escrow the correct asset when an order is placed.
- Encrypt amount and direction with a common submission path.
- Validate encrypted inputs and bind them to the correct account, contract and chain.
- Define safe handling of insufficient balances, invalid directions and repeated submissions without creating unnecessary information leaks.
- Specify order limits and batch size according to measured gas and FHE costs.

### Batch and settlement lifecycle

- Implement a clear state machine for open, closed, awaiting decryption, ready to settle, settled, credited and recoverable states.
- Perform matching under encryption and reveal only values required for the public Uniswap leg.
- Verify decryption proofs and bind them to the correct round.
- Execute the residual against the configured Uniswap pool with enforced bounds.
- Credit outputs confidentially and prevent duplicate credits.
- Define what happens if the residual is zero, a swap fails, liquidity is insufficient or a decryption request stalls.

### Recovery and administration

- Avoid a design in which a single unavailable keeper can permanently lock funds.
- Define permissionless or redundant settlement paths where feasible.
- Provide bounded recovery or refunds for stalled rounds without enabling theft, double spending or privacy-breaking shortcuts.
- Minimize privileged roles; document any remaining roles, upgrade powers and emergency controls.
- Specify upgrade and migration procedures before real funds are accepted.

**Deliverable:** contracts, deployment scripts, invariants, documented trust assumptions and a reproducible local test environment.

## Phase 4: Establish the WETH/custom-token pool

1. Deploy the custom ERC-20 and confidential wrappers on a test network.
2. Deploy the batch protocol configured for WETH, the custom token and one Uniswap pool.
3. Initialize the pool and provide appropriate liquidity.
4. Record and validate the chain ID, contract versions, token and wrapper addresses, pool key, fee tier, deployment block and protocol parameters.
5. Verify deployed bytecode and configuration against the build artifacts.
6. Run a funded keeper or decentralized settlement service with monitoring and a documented operating procedure.

The first deployment should be on Sepolia. A production deployment follows only after the pricing design, security review and operational requirements are satisfied.

## Phase 5: Integrate Prawl’s wallet and privacy infrastructure

Create a dedicated swap module rather than embedding the ETHGlobal standalone UI.

### Signing and transport

- Use Prawl’s unlocked account and in-memory signing lifecycle; do not require MetaMask.
- Use the wallet’s existing transaction sender and receipt handling.
- Route blockchain reads and broadcasts through Anon-RPC with appropriate circuit isolation.
- Review all FHE SDK and gateway requests. Integrate them with the wallet’s privacy transport where technically possible, and disclose any service that receives identifiable metadata.
- Clear encryption and decryption session material when the wallet locks or changes.

### Funding from RAILGUN

- Determine whether Prawl can safely unshield WETH and fund the confidential vault through an atomic or staged flow.
- Verify the vault owner, encrypted-input signer, gas payer and account that can later decrypt and withdraw.
- Implement the safest supported flow and show its fees and on-chain visibility before submission.
- If movement from RAILGUN requires a public account, make that step explicit rather than presenting it as a private bridge.
- Handle the custom token separately, including whether RAILGUN supports its shielding and spending on the target chain.

### Balances and recovery

- Read public token balances and encrypted vault balances from the actual deployment.
- Decrypt only the user’s authorized balances.
- Recover order and round state from chain after a reload, device restore or interrupted session.
- Store local activity using the wallet’s existing encrypted storage; do not store plaintext secrets.
- Treat uncertain transaction outcomes as unknown and avoid blindly resubmitting them.

## Phase 6: Complete the swap dashboard

Replace the current disabled dashboard with a usable interface.

**Before submitting**

- Pair and direction.
- Available balance in each relevant custody location.
- Input amount, estimated output and price.
- Pool fee, protocol fee, estimated gas and expected settlement conditions.
- User-controlled execution protection where supported.
- Explicit warnings for public funding or exit and for limited batch privacy.

**During the swap**

- Funding and approval progress.
- Encryption and order-submission progress.
- Transaction confirmation and batch membership.
- Round status: waiting, closing, decrypting, settling and crediting.
- Clear handling of delayed settlement and unknown transaction outcomes.

**After settlement**

- The actual credited output after the user decrypts their balance.
- Confidential vault balances and transaction history.
- Exit to the public account and, when supported, re-shielding into RAILGUN.
- Recovery guidance when any step requires user action.

The dashboard must derive prices, balances and statuses from real data; unavailable information should be labelled as unavailable rather than estimated or invented.

## Phase 7: Verification and security

### Automated testing

- Contract unit and invariant tests for custody, conservation of value, permissions, accounting, rounding, decryption proofs and lifecycle transitions.
- Adversarial tests for price changes, sandwiching, stale prices, zero or insufficient liquidity, malicious tokens, duplicate calls and keeper failures.
- Fuzz and stateful testing across multiple users, directions, batch sizes and settlement sequences.
- Wallet tests for signing, encryption, network routing, lock/unlock, reload recovery and transaction failure states.
- Regression tests for existing Prawl features.

### End-to-end testing

Run realistic multi-user tests on Sepolia:

1. Fund users and provide pool liquidity.
2. Move supported assets from Prawl into the swap vault.
3. Submit encrypted orders in both directions.
4. Exercise matched, partially matched and residual-only rounds.
5. Settle through the actual Uniswap pool.
6. Verify each user’s confidential credit and execution bounds.
7. Exit and re-shield supported assets.
8. Restart the wallet and keeper at each stage and verify recovery.
9. Inspect on-chain events and network traffic against the documented privacy claims.

### Independent review

Before production use, obtain independent reviews of:

- Smart contracts and economic design.
- FHE integration and privacy properties.
- Wallet key handling and transaction flows.
- Infrastructure, keeper operations and incident response.

Resolve findings, retest fixes and publish the final trust and privacy assumptions.

## Phase 8: Production launch and operations

- Deploy verified contracts and the single pool with reviewed configuration.
- Establish sustainable liquidity and monitor price, liquidity depth and settlement failures.
- Run redundant settlement infrastructure with alerts for stalled rounds, gateway failures, insufficient gas and failed exits.
- Define incident response, maintenance, upgrade and migration procedures.
- Publish user documentation describing custody locations, fees, delays, recovery and what remains public.
- Launch only after the security review and end-to-end acceptance tests pass.

## Definition of done

The integration is complete when a user can open Prawl, select **Swap**, fund the confidential swap balance using the supported wallet flow, submit an encrypted order, receive the correctly settled output, and exit or re-shield it—all with accurate balances and status throughout. Existing wallet functions must pass their regression tests, and the deployed system must have verified contracts, functioning settlement infrastructure, documented privacy limits and completed independent security review.

A production-ready result cannot be established solely by implementing the dashboard or reproducing the hackathon demo. It depends on the final pricing design, real deployed infrastructure, liquidity, and security verification as well.

I’ll start by checking the current wallet and swap prototype against the plan, then build the integration in stages. The first priority is a sound contract and funding design: the prototype’s fixed 1:1 matching cannot safely price a general WETH/token pool.

    // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
    pragma solidity ^0.8.27;
    
    import {FHE, ebool, euint8, euint64, euint128, externalEuint8, externalEuint64} from "@fhevm/solidity/lib/FHE.sol";
    import {ZamaEthereumConfig} from "@fhevm/solidity/config/ZamaConfig.sol";
    
    import {IERC20} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol";
    import {SafeERC20} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol";
    
    import {IERC7984Receiver} from "@openzeppelin/confidential-contracts/contracts/interfaces/IERC7984Receiver.sol";
    import {ERC7984ERC20Wrapper} from "@openzeppelin/confidential-contracts/contracts/token/ERC7984/extensions/ERC7984ERC20Wrapper.sol";
    
    import {IUniversalRouter} from "@uniswap/universal-router/contracts/interfaces/IUniversalRouter.sol";
    import {Commands} from "@uniswap/universal-router/contracts/libraries/Commands.sol";
    import {IPermit2} from "@uniswap/permit2/src/interfaces/IPermit2.sol";
    import {IV4Router} from "@uniswap/v4-periphery/src/interfaces/IV4Router.sol";
    import {Actions} from "@uniswap/v4-periphery/src/libraries/Actions.sol";
    import {Currency} from "@uniswap/v4-core/src/types/Currency.sol";
    import {PoolKey, IHooks} from "@uniswap/v4-core/src/types/PoolKey.sol";
    
    /// @title VeilBatchSwap v3 — fully confidential batched swaps.
    ///
    /// Privacy model (honest version):
    ///  * Funding: a user moves confidential tokens (ERC-7984 wrappers) into this
    ///    vault once. That funding tx is public (wallet, token, amount) — it does
    ///    NOT hide the wallet↔funding link. Later orders draw on the funded
    ///    balance, so the funding amount does not reveal order sizes.
    ///  * Orders: direction AND amount are encrypted (euint8/euint64). One shared
    ///    order entry point for both assets; the order event carries no plaintext
    ///    direction or amount. The batch holds both c-tokens, so the entry path
    ///    does not reveal which side the user chose.
    ///  * Rounds: the two directional totals, the matched amount and every user's
    ///    order stay encrypted forever. Only the NET residual and WHICH side is in
    ///    surplus are publicly decryptable (needed to run the Uniswap leg).
    ///  * Payouts: credited back into the user's confidential vault balance —
    ///    never sent as plain ERC-20 by the settlement flow. Exits to plain
    ///    tokens are a separate, intentionally-public step.
    ///  * Public by design: relayer, round timing, token pair, the net Uniswap
    ///    trade, and (honestly) WHICH ADDRESSES ORDERED in a round. Batching
    ///    blurs per-order patterns; it is not an anonymity guarantee.
    ///
    /// Matching: greedy pairwise netting inside FHE. For every pair of orders
    /// with opposite (encrypted) directions, m = min(remaining_i, remaining_j) is
    /// matched 1:1; the leftovers form the round's net residual. Because fhEVM
    /// has no encrypted division, pro-rata division is avoided: deficit-side
    /// payouts are exactly the matched amount, and the surplus side's share of
    /// the Uniswap output is residual × swapOutput / net — division by the
    /// PUBLIC net only (FHE mul/div by clear scalars).
    contract VeilBatchSwap is ZamaEthereumConfig, IERC7984Receiver {
        using SafeERC20 for IERC20;
    
        uint256 public constant VERSION = 3;
    
        uint8 public constant DIR_A_TO_B = 1; // sell c-tokenA, receive c-tokenB
        uint8 public constant DIR_B_TO_A = 2;
    
        /// Max orders per round — bounds the O(n²) encrypted netting gas.
        uint256 public constant MAX_ORDERS_PER_ROUND = 6;
    
        struct RoundState {
            euint64 aToB; // encrypted total sold A→B (c-tokenA units)
            euint64 bToA; // encrypted total sold B→A (c-tokenB units)
            euint64 netEnc; // encrypted net residual (decrypted copy is public)
            euint8 surplusEnc; // encrypted surplus side (decrypted copy is public)
            bool closed;
            bool prepared;
            bool executed;
            uint64 netSwapped; // clear net residual, after gateway decryption
            uint8 surplusSide; // clear surplus side (1 = A, 2 = B)
            uint256 minSwapOutput; // slippage floor in c-units, set on-chain
            bytes32 unwrapHandle; // pending async unwrap request for the net
            uint64 swapOutput; // Uniswap leg output, c-units of the other token
        }
    
        IUniversalRouter public immutable router;
        IPermit2 public immutable permit2;
        ERC7984ERC20Wrapper public immutable cTokenA;
        ERC7984ERC20Wrapper public immutable cTokenB;
        PoolKey public poolKey;
        uint256 public immutable minRoundDuration;
        uint256 public immutable minDistinctSubmitters;
        address public immutable settler;
    
        uint256 public currentRound = 1;
        uint256 public lastUpdateTime;
        uint256 public distinctSubmitters;
    
        // Confidential funding vault: [user][0 = asset A, 1 = asset B].
        mapping(address => mapping(uint8 => euint64)) private _funded;
    
        // Orders per round.
        mapping(uint256 => mapping(address => euint64)) private _orderAmount;
        mapping(uint256 => mapping(address => euint8)) private _orderDir;
        mapping(uint256 => mapping(address => euint64)) private _orderMatched;
        mapping(uint256 => mapping(address => euint64)) private _orderResidual;
        mapping(uint256 => mapping(address => bool)) private _orderCredited;
        mapping(uint256 => address[]) private _orderers;
        mapping(uint256 => mapping(address => bool)) private _isOrderer;
    
        mapping(uint256 => RoundState) public rounds;
    
        event Funded(address indexed user, uint8 indexed asset); // asset: 0 = A, 1 = B
        event OrderSubmitted(uint256 indexed round, address indexed user); // no direction, no amount
        event RoundClosed(uint256 indexed round);
        event RoundPrepared(uint256 indexed round, uint64 netSwapped, bytes32 unwrapHandle);
        event RoundSettled(uint256 indexed round, uint8 surplusSide, uint64 netSwapped, uint64 swapOutput);
        event OrderCredited(uint256 indexed round, address indexed user); // no amounts
    
        error RoundNotOpen();
        error OrderAlreadySubmitted();
        error TooManyOrders();
        error TooEarlyForNextRound();
        error NotEnoughSubmitters();
        error RoundAlreadyClosed();
        error RoundNotClosed();
        error RoundAlreadySettled();
        error RoundNotPrepared();
        error UnwrapMismatch(uint64 provided, uint64 expected);
        error UnknownDirection();
        error UntrustedToken();
        error NoOrder();
        error AlreadyCredited();
        error SlippageExceeded(uint256 swapOutput, uint256 minSwapOutput);
        error NotSettler();
        error SwapOutputOverflow();
    
        constructor(
            address router_,
            address permit2_,
            address cTokenA_,
            address cTokenB_,
            PoolKey memory poolKey_,
            uint256 minRoundDuration_,
            uint256 minDistinctSubmitters_,
            address settler_
        ) {
            router = IUniversalRouter(router_);
            permit2 = IPermit2(permit2_);
            cTokenA = ERC7984ERC20Wrapper(cTokenA_);
            cTokenB = ERC7984ERC20Wrapper(cTokenB_);
            poolKey = poolKey_;
            minRoundDuration = minRoundDuration_;
            minDistinctSubmitters = minDistinctSubmitters_;
            require(settler_ != address(0), "settler is zero");
            settler = settler_;
            lastUpdateTime = block.timestamp;
        }
    
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Confidential funding vault
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    
        /// @notice ERC-7984 receiver hook. Empty data (or a single 0x00 byte)
        /// funds the sender's confidential balance of the transferred asset.
        /// Funding is public by design (see the header); the credited amount is
        /// stored encrypted so it never reveals order sizes.
        function onConfidentialTransferReceived(
            address, // operator — always the wrapper
            address from,
            euint64 eAmount,
            bytes calldata data
        ) external returns (ebool success) {
            if (msg.sender != address(cTokenA) && msg.sender != address(cTokenB)) {
                success = FHE.asEbool(false);
                FHE.allow(success, msg.sender);
                return success;
            }
            if (data.length != 0 && !(data.length == 1 && data[0] == 0x00)) {
                success = FHE.asEbool(false);
                FHE.allow(success, msg.sender);
                return success;
            }
            uint8 asset = msg.sender == address(cTokenA) ? 0 : 1;
    
            euint64 previous = FHE.isInitialized(_funded[from][asset]) ? _funded[from][asset] : FHE.asEuint64(0);
            euint64 updated = FHE.add(previous, eAmount);
            // Reject zero-value and uint64-overflowing funding without exposing
            // either condition (the wrapper refunds when success is false).
            ebool accepted = FHE.gt(updated, previous);
            updated = FHE.select(accepted, updated, previous);
            FHE.allowThis(updated);
            FHE.allow(updated, from);
            _funded[from][asset] = updated;
            // Initialize the other side too, so the user can always decrypt both
            // balances (uninitialized handles cannot be user-decrypted).
            uint8 other = asset == 0 ? uint8(1) : uint8(0);
            if (!FHE.isInitialized(_funded[from][other])) {
                _funded[from][other] = FHE.asEuint64(0);
                FHE.allowThis(_funded[from][other]);
                FHE.allow(_funded[from][other], from);
            }
    
            emit Funded(from, asset);
            success = accepted;
            FHE.allow(success, msg.sender);
            return success;
        }
    
        /// @notice Encrypted handle of a user's funded balance (for self-decryption).
        function fundedBalance(address user, uint8 asset) external view returns (euint64) {
            return _funded[user][asset];
        }
    
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Orders — encrypted direction and amount, one shared entry point
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    
        /// @notice Submit an order from the funded confidential balance.
        /// @param encAmount encrypted order size in c-token units (6 dp).
        /// @param encDirection encrypted direction: 1 = sell A→B, 2 = sell B→A.
        /// Both values come from ONE client-side encrypted input; the same input
        /// proof covers both handles. Neither value is ever public.
        function submitOrder(externalEuint64 encAmount, bytes calldata amountProof, externalEuint8 encDirection, bytes calldata directionProof) external {
            RoundState storage round = rounds[currentRound];
            require(!round.closed, RoundNotOpen());
            require(euint8.unwrap(_orderDir[currentRound][msg.sender]) == 0, OrderAlreadySubmitted());
            require(_orderers[currentRound].length < MAX_ORDERS_PER_ROUND, TooManyOrders());
    
            euint64 amt = FHE.fromExternal(encAmount, amountProof);
            euint8 dir = FHE.fromExternal(encDirection, directionProof);
    
            // Debit the funded balance of the chosen asset. The direction stays
            // encrypted: each side's debit is the amount masked by the direction
            // bit, and an underfunded order is silently zeroed (OZ pattern) so
            // the revert-or-not channel never leaks the balance comparison.
            ebool isA = FHE.eq(dir, DIR_A_TO_B);
            ebool isB = FHE.eq(dir, DIR_B_TO_A);
            euint64 debitA = FHE.select(isA, amt, FHE.asEuint64(0));
            euint64 debitB = FHE.select(isB, amt, FHE.asEuint64(0));
    
            euint64 balA = FHE.isInitialized(_funded[msg.sender][0]) ? _funded[msg.sender][0] : FHE.asEuint64(0);
            euint64 balB = FHE.isInitialized(_funded[msg.sender][1]) ? _funded[msg.sender][1] : FHE.asEuint64(0);
            debitA = FHE.select(FHE.ge(balA, debitA), debitA, FHE.asEuint64(0));
            debitB = FHE.select(FHE.ge(balB, debitB), debitB, FHE.asEuint64(0));
    
            euint64 newBalA = FHE.sub(balA, debitA);
            euint64 newBalB = FHE.sub(balB, debitB);
            FHE.allowThis(newBalA);
            FHE.allowThis(newBalB);
            FHE.allow(newBalA, msg.sender);
            FHE.allow(newBalB, msg.sender);
            _funded[msg.sender][0] = newBalA;
            _funded[msg.sender][1] = newBalB;
    
            // Round totals (encrypted forever).
            euint64 prevA = FHE.isInitialized(round.aToB) ? round.aToB : FHE.asEuint64(0);
            euint64 prevB = FHE.isInitialized(round.bToA) ? round.bToA : FHE.asEuint64(0);
            round.aToB = FHE.add(prevA, debitA);
            round.bToA = FHE.add(prevB, debitB);
            FHE.allowThis(round.aToB);
            FHE.allowThis(round.bToA);
    
            _orderAmount[currentRound][msg.sender] = FHE.add(debitA, debitB); // exactly one side is non-zero
            _orderDir[currentRound][msg.sender] = dir;
            FHE.allowThis(_orderAmount[currentRound][msg.sender]);
            FHE.allowThis(dir);
    
            if (!_isOrderer[currentRound][msg.sender]) {
                _orderers[currentRound].push(msg.sender);
                _isOrderer[currentRound][msg.sender] = true;
                _isOrderer[currentRound][msg.sender] = true;
                ++distinctSubmitters;
            }
    
            emit OrderSubmitted(currentRound, msg.sender);
        }
    
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Phase 1 — close the round: encrypted netting, nothing decrypts
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    
        /// @notice Close the round and run the greedy pairwise netting entirely
        /// inside FHE. Per-order matched/residual amounts are computed and stored
        /// encrypted. Only the NET residual and the surplus SIDE become publicly
        /// decryptable — never the two totals, never the matched amount, never
        /// any user's order.
        function closeRound() external {
            require(block.timestamp >= lastUpdateTime + minRoundDuration, TooEarlyForNextRound());
            require(distinctSubmitters >= minDistinctSubmitters, NotEnoughSubmitters());
            RoundState storage round = rounds[currentRound];
            require(!round.closed, RoundAlreadyClosed());
            round.closed = true;
    
            address[] memory orderers = _orderers[currentRound];
            uint256 n = orderers.length;
    
            // Greedy pairwise netting: for every opposite-direction pair, match
            // min(remaining_i, remaining_j) 1:1. Sequential encrypted state, no
            // branching on encrypted values.
            euint64[] memory remaining = new euint64[](n);
            euint64[] memory matched = new euint64[](n);
            for (uint256 i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
                remaining[i] = _orderAmount[currentRound][orderers[i]];
                matched[i] = FHE.asEuint64(0);
            }
            for (uint256 i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
                for (uint256 j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
                    ebool opposite = FHE.not(FHE.eq(_orderDir[currentRound][orderers[i]], _orderDir[currentRound][orderers[j]]));
                    euint64 m = FHE.min(remaining[i], remaining[j]);
                    m = FHE.select(opposite, m, FHE.asEuint64(0));
                    remaining[i] = FHE.sub(remaining[i], m);
                    remaining[j] = FHE.sub(remaining[j], m);
                    matched[i] = FHE.add(matched[i], m);
                    matched[j] = FHE.add(matched[j], m);
                }
            }
            for (uint256 i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
                _orderMatched[currentRound][orderers[i]] = matched[i];
                _orderResidual[currentRound][orderers[i]] = remaining[i];
                FHE.allowThis(matched[i]);
                FHE.allowThis(remaining[i]);
            }
    
            // Round totals for the net computation (stay encrypted).
            if (!FHE.isInitialized(round.aToB)) {
                round.aToB = FHE.asEuint64(0);
                FHE.allowThis(round.aToB);
            }
            if (!FHE.isInitialized(round.bToA)) {
                round.bToA = FHE.asEuint64(0);
                FHE.allowThis(round.bToA);
            }
            euint64 net = FHE.sub(FHE.max(round.aToB, round.bToA), FHE.min(round.aToB, round.bToA));
            ebool aSurplus = FHE.ge(round.aToB, round.bToA);
            euint8 surplus = FHE.select(aSurplus, FHE.asEuint8(1), FHE.asEuint8(2));
            round.netEnc = net;
            round.surplusEnc = surplus;
            FHE.allowThis(net);
            FHE.allowThis(surplus);
            // The ONLY two values made publicly decryptable in the whole protocol.
            FHE.makePubliclyDecryptable(net);
            FHE.makePubliclyDecryptable(surplus);
    
            emit RoundClosed(currentRound);
    
            currentRound++;
            distinctSubmitters = 0;
            lastUpdateTime = block.timestamp;
        }
    
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Phase 2 — prepare (settler) then execute (permissionless)
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    
        /// @param clearValues abi.encode(uint64 net, uint8 surplusSide) — the
        ///        gateway decryption of the round's net residual and surplus side.
        /// @notice Verifies the gateway signatures, records the (public by design)
        ///         net/surplus, sets the on-chain slippage floor, and opens the
        ///         async unwrap request for the net residual.
        function prepareSettle(uint256 roundId, bytes calldata clearValues, bytes calldata decryptionProof) external {
            if (msg.sender != settler) revert NotSettler();
            RoundState storage round = rounds[roundId];
            require(round.closed, RoundNotClosed());
            require(!round.prepared && !round.executed, RoundAlreadySettled());
    
            (uint64 net, uint8 surplusSide) = abi.decode(clearValues, (uint64, uint8));
            require(surplusSide == DIR_A_TO_B || surplusSide == DIR_B_TO_A, UnknownDirection());
    
            bytes32[] memory cts = new bytes32[](2);
            cts[0] = euint64.unwrap(round.netEnc);
            cts[1] = euint8.unwrap(round.surplusEnc);
            FHE.checkSignatures(cts, clearValues, decryptionProof);
    
            round.prepared = true;
            round.netSwapped = net;
            round.surplusSide = surplusSide;
            // Slippage floor computed on-chain: 90% of the net input (the batch
            // price is 1:1 internally, so the net leg should not lose >10%).
            round.minSwapOutput = (net * 9000) / 10000;
    
            bytes32 unwrapHandle = 0;
            if (net > 0) {
                ERC7984ERC20Wrapper surplusWrapper = surplusSide == DIR_A_TO_B ? cTokenA : cTokenB;
                euint64 netEnc = FHE.asEuint64(net);
                FHE.allowTransient(netEnc, address(surplusWrapper));
                // _burn may re-key the handle; the returned value is the one the
                // wrapper made publicly decryptable and registered as the request.
                euint64 unwrapped = surplusWrapper.unwrap(address(this), address(this), netEnc);
                unwrapHandle = FHE.toBytes32(unwrapped);
                round.unwrapHandle = unwrapHandle;
            }
            emit RoundPrepared(roundId, net, unwrapHandle);
        }
    
        /// @param unwrapCleartext the gateway-decrypted value of the unwrap request
        /// @notice Permissionless: finalizes the unwrap, swaps the net leg on
        ///         Uniswap v4, wraps the output back, and enforces the slippage
        ///         guard. The per-order encrypted matched/residual amounts are
        ///         untouched here — payouts credit confidential vault balances.
        function executeSettle(uint256 roundId, uint64 unwrapCleartext, bytes calldata decryptionProof) external {
            RoundState storage round = rounds[roundId];
            require(round.prepared, RoundNotPrepared());
            require(!round.executed, RoundAlreadySettled());
    
            uint64 swapOutput = 0;
            if (round.netSwapped > 0) {
                if (unwrapCleartext != round.netSwapped) revert UnwrapMismatch(unwrapCleartext, round.netSwapped);
                ERC7984ERC20Wrapper surplusWrapper = round.surplusSide == DIR_A_TO_B ? cTokenA : cTokenB;
                ERC7984ERC20Wrapper outputWrapper = round.surplusSide == DIR_A_TO_B ? cTokenB : cTokenA;
                uint256 rateIn = surplusWrapper.rate();
                uint256 rateOut = outputWrapper.rate();
    
                surplusWrapper.finalizeUnwrap(euint64.wrap(round.unwrapHandle), unwrapCleartext, decryptionProof);
    
                IERC20 inputToken = IERC20(surplusWrapper.underlying());
                IERC20 outputToken = IERC20(outputWrapper.underlying());
                uint256 underlyingIn = uint256(round.netSwapped) * rateIn;
                uint256 amountOut = _swapExactInput(inputToken, outputToken, underlyingIn);
                uint256 outputInWrapperUnits = amountOut / rateOut;
                if (outputInWrapperUnits > type(uint64).max) revert SwapOutputOverflow();
                swapOutput = uint64(outputInWrapperUnits);
                outputToken.forceApprove(address(outputWrapper), amountOut);
                outputWrapper.wrap(address(this), amountOut);
            }
    
            if (swapOutput < round.minSwapOutput) {
                revert SlippageExceeded(swapOutput, round.minSwapOutput);
            }
            round.executed = true;
            round.swapOutput = swapOutput;
            emit RoundSettled(roundId, round.surplusSide, round.netSwapped, swapOutput);
        }
    
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Payouts — credited to confidential vault balances, permissionless
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    
        /// @notice Credit an orderer's payout back into their confidential vault
        /// balance (never plain ERC-20 — that would expose the payout amount and
        /// recipient). Permissionless: anyone may credit anyone; the credit always
        /// lands in the orderer's own encrypted balance. Single-credit enforced
        /// with a flag; the credited amounts stay encrypted.
        ///
        /// Payout math (no encrypted division):
        ///   deficit side: payout = matched_i                       (1:1 match)
        ///   surplus side: payout = matched_i + residual_i × swapOutput / net
        /// where swapOutput and net are public by design; the mul/div use clear
        /// scalars, which fhEVM supports.
        function creditOrder(uint256 roundId, address user) external {
            RoundState storage round = rounds[roundId];
            require(round.executed, RoundNotClosed());
            euint8 dir = _orderDir[roundId][user];
            require(euint8.unwrap(dir) != 0, NoOrder());
            require(!_orderCredited[roundId][user], AlreadyCredited());
            _orderCredited[roundId][user] = true;
    
            ebool isA = FHE.eq(dir, DIR_A_TO_B); // sold A → receives B
            ebool onSurplusSide = FHE.eq(dir, FHE.asEuint8(round.surplusSide));
    
            euint64 payout = _orderMatched[roundId][user];
            if (round.netSwapped > 0) {
                // share of the Uniswap output: residual_i × swapOutput / net
                euint64 swapShare = FHE.div(
                    FHE.mul(_orderResidual[roundId][user], round.swapOutput),
                    round.netSwapped
                );
                payout = FHE.add(payout, FHE.select(onSurplusSide, swapShare, FHE.asEuint64(0)));
            }
    
            // Sold A → the payout lands in the B balance, and vice versa.
            euint64 toA = FHE.select(isA, FHE.asEuint64(0), payout);
            euint64 toB = FHE.select(isA, payout, FHE.asEuint64(0));
    
            euint64 balA = FHE.isInitialized(_funded[user][0]) ? _funded[user][0] : FHE.asEuint64(0);
            euint64 balB = FHE.isInitialized(_funded[user][1]) ? _funded[user][1] : FHE.asEuint64(0);
            euint64 newA = FHE.add(balA, toA);
            euint64 newB = FHE.add(balB, toB);
            FHE.allowThis(newA);
            FHE.allowThis(newB);
            FHE.allow(newA, user);
            FHE.allow(newB, user);
            _funded[user][0] = newA;
            _funded[user][1] = newB;
    
            emit OrderCredited(roundId, user);
        }
    
        /// @notice Exit: unwrap part of the caller's confidential balance to plain
        /// ERC-20 in their wallet. Intentionally public (funding/exit leg — see
        /// the header). The amount is caller-supplied and capped by the caller's
        /// own encrypted balance (silently zeroed if larger, OZ pattern). The
        /// unwrap is async: anyone finalizes it and the underlying arrives.
        function exit(uint8 asset, externalEuint64 encAmount, bytes calldata proof) external {
            require(asset <= 1, UnknownDirection());
            euint64 amt = FHE.fromExternal(encAmount, proof);
            euint64 bal = FHE.isInitialized(_funded[msg.sender][asset]) ? _funded[msg.sender][asset] : FHE.asEuint64(0);
            amt = FHE.select(FHE.ge(bal, amt), amt, FHE.asEuint64(0));
            euint64 newBal = FHE.sub(bal, amt);
            FHE.allowThis(newBal);
            FHE.allow(newBal, msg.sender);
            _funded[msg.sender][asset] = newBal;
    
            ERC7984ERC20Wrapper w = asset == 0 ? cTokenA : cTokenB;
            FHE.allowTransient(amt, address(w));
            w.unwrap(address(this), msg.sender, amt);
        }
    
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Uniswap v4 swap — net leg only
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    
        function _swapExactInput(IERC20 tokenIn, IERC20 tokenOut, uint256 amountIn) internal virtual returns (uint256 amountOut) {
            Currency inputCurrency = Currency.wrap(address(tokenIn));
            Currency outputCurrency = Currency.wrap(address(tokenOut));
            bool zeroForOne = Currency.unwrap(poolKey.currency0) == address(tokenIn);
    
            bytes memory commands = abi.encodePacked(uint8(Commands.V4_SWAP));
            bytes[] memory params = new bytes[](3);
            params[0] = abi.encode(
                IV4Router.ExactInputSingleParams({
                    poolKey: poolKey,
                    zeroForOne: zeroForOne,
                    amountIn: uint128(amountIn),
                    amountOutMinimum: 0, // guarded below via balance check + slippage floor
                    hookData: bytes('')
                })
            );
            params[1] = abi.encode(inputCurrency, amountIn); // SETTLE_ALL
            params[2] = abi.encode(outputCurrency, address(this), uint256(0)); // TAKE
            bytes[] memory inputs = new bytes[](1);
            inputs[0] = abi.encode(
                abi.encodePacked(
                    uint8(Actions.SWAP_EXACT_IN_SINGLE),
                    uint8(Actions.SETTLE_ALL),
                    uint8(Actions.TAKE)
                ),
                params
            );
    
            uint256 balanceBefore = tokenOut.balanceOf(address(this));
            tokenIn.forceApprove(address(permit2), amountIn);
            permit2.approve(
                address(tokenIn),
                address(router),
                uint160(amountIn),
                uint48(block.timestamp + 30 minutes)
            );
            router.execute(commands, inputs, block.timestamp + 30 minutes);
            amountOut = tokenOut.balanceOf(address(this)) - balanceBefore;
        }
    }

    /**
     * Prawl's Anon-RPC client.
     *
     * Every request (JSON-RPC read, JSON-RPC batch, write, HTTP GET/POST) goes
     * through one private path, `#send`, which:
     *   1. checks the identity handle came from this client's registry, and that
     *      the URL and body name no other registered identity;
     *   2. takes a slot from the scheduler (per-identity and global limits);
     *   3. acquires a ready worker (the boot deadline lives in WorkerHost);
     *   4. sends one attempt with per-request deadlines and a circuit key
     *      `${identity}@${provider}#${generation}`;
     *   5. on a retryable failure (timeouts, CONNECTION, TLS, HTTP_REQUEST,
     *      IO_ERROR, HTTP 403/429/5xx, or an exit-side Arti kind) retries once on
     *      a fresh circuit, then, if allowed, fails over once to the next
     *      provider in the fixed order. A destination answer such as
     *      RemoteConnectionRefused keeps the circuit and skips straight to the
     *      next provider. Writes get exactly one attempt. JSON-RPC errors are
     *      answers and are never retried;
     *   6. emits one trace per attempt.
     *
     * Reads and GETs carry `anonOptions.idempotent: true`, so a keep-alive worker
     * may resend them once on a fresh connection if a pooled connection died
     * under them. Writes never carry it, so no worker can send them twice.
     * Slow reads (deep eth_getLogs, raised HTTP deadlines) also carry
     * `reusedHeadersTimeoutMs: 0`, so fork v3's 5 s stall deadline on reused
     * connections never cuts a legitimately slow answer.
     *
     * Time to first answer (2026-11-06): `start()` pre-warms with the shared head
     * read itself (public scope, no address), and `blockTag()` never waits for a
     * head: with no fresh head it returns "latest", so an identity's first batch
     * goes out the moment the worker is ready. At boot Arti has no circuit yet and
     * hands the first one (about 0.8 s after ready) to whichever isolated request
     * asked first; the others wait 2-3 s for their own. So with `bootPriority`,
     * head reads that reach the worker while an account or stealth identity's
     * first request is still unanswered wait (at most `headDeferMs`) until it has
     * its answer: the user's data gets the first circuit. `warm(identity)` opens
     * an identity's own circuit with a neutral eth_chainId before its first read.
     *
     * There is no direct-network code path: the only thing that sends bytes is
     * the worker handed out by WorkerHost.
     */
    import { blockRefOf, readKey, ReadCache } from "./cache";
    import { fetchWithDeadlines, safeHost, type RawResponse } from "./deadlines";
    import { AnonRpcError, EXIT_SIDE_TOR_KINDS, isRetryableStatus, retryAction, TIMEOUT_CODES, TRANSPORT_CODES } from "./errors";
    import { IdentityRegistry, type Identity } from "./identity";
    import {
      DEFAULT_POLICY,
      MAINNET,
      providersFor,
      READ_METHODS,
      routeFor,
      validatePolicy,
      WRITE_METHODS,
      type ProviderPolicy,
    } from "./providers";
    import { Scheduler, type SchedulerOptions } from "./scheduler";
    import { DEFAULT_DEADLINES, type AnonOptions, type Deadlines, type RequestKind, type Trace, type TraceSink } from "./types";
    import type { WorkerHost } from "./worker-host";
    
    /** Sent on every request instead of the browser's real user agent. */
    export const ANON_USER_AGENT = "Mozilla/5.0";
    
    export interface ClientOptions {
      host: WorkerHost;
      policy?: ProviderPolicy;
      deadlines?: Partial<Deadlines>;
      scheduler?: SchedulerOptions;
      /** Fail over to the next provider after a request failed twice. Disclose this to users. */
      failover?: boolean;
      /** Exact hosts (host[:port]) that http() may reach. */
      httpHosts?: readonly string[];
      onTrace?: TraceSink;
      userAgent?: string;
      cacheEntries?: number;
      /** Blocks behind head treated as final, per chain (default 96). */
      finalityDepth?: Record<number, number>;
      headTtlMs?: number;
      /** Chain whose first read provider gets the neutral pre-warm request; false disables. */
      prewarmChainId?: number | false;
      /**
       * What the pre-warm sends. "head" (default): the chain's shared head read
       * (eth_blockNumber on the public `head:<chain>` scope), so the warm-up is
       * also useful work and, on a keep-alive worker, leaves a connection that
       * later head refreshes reuse. "chainId": the earlier eth_chainId on a
       * throwaway key (kept to reproduce the 2026-11-06 baseline).
       */
      prewarm?: "head" | "chainId";
      /** blockTag() pins to a cached head at most this old (default 60 s); older or none: "latest". */
      headMaxStaleMs?: number;
      /** warm() sends at most one neutral request per identity and provider within this window (default 50 s). */
      warmWindowMs?: number;
      /**
       * Boot priority (default true): until the first account/stealth request has
       * an answer (headers or failure), head reads wait for it, at most
       * `headDeferMs` (default 2 s), so the identity's request claims the first
       * circuit. Only head reads are deferred; nothing is reordered after that.
       */
      bootPriority?: boolean;
      headDeferMs?: number;
      random?: () => number;
      now?: () => number;
    }
    
    export interface Target {
      id: string;
      url: string;
    }
    
    export interface HttpRequest {
      url: string;
      method?: "GET" | "POST";
      headers?: Record<string, string>;
      body?: string;
      /** "write" for anything that must not be sent twice (proof submission). */
      kind?: "read" | "write";
      deadlines?: Partial<Deadlines>;
      signal?: AbortSignal;
      /** Return non-2xx responses instead of throwing (after retries). */
      passHttpErrors?: boolean;
    }
    
    export interface HttpResult {
      status: number;
      statusText: string;
      headers: Headers;
      body: Uint8Array;
      url: string;
    }
    
    export type BatchItem = { result: unknown } | { error: AnonRpcError };
    
    interface SendSpec {
      kind: RequestKind;
      identity: Identity;
      label: string;
      targets: Target[];
      method: "GET" | "POST";
      headers: Record<string, string>;
      body?: string;
      deadlines: Deadlines;

    export default config;
    /**
     * Identity handles. Every request names exactly one identity, and the
     * isolation key is derived from that identity, never from the flow that
     * happens to be running. Two flows about the same address share a circuit;
     * two addresses never do.
     *
     * Handles can only be minted by an IdentityRegistry, and a client accepts
     * only handles from its own registry, so a caller cannot forge a key string
     * or pass a list of identities.
     */
    import { AnonRpcError } from "./errors";
    
    export type IdentityKind = "account" | "stealth" | "tx" | "public" | "ephemeral";
    
    const ISSUED = Symbol("anon-rpc.identity");
    
    export interface Identity {
      readonly kind: IdentityKind;
      /** Stable base key, e.g. `acct:0xabc…`. The circuit key adds provider and generation. */
      readonly key: string;
      /** Short label for traces. Never the full address. */
      readonly label: string;
      /**
       * Lower-case hex without 0x (40 chars for an address, 64 for a tx hash), or
       * undefined for public and ephemeral scopes. Used by the cross-identity guard.
       */
      readonly subject: string | undefined;
      readonly [ISSUED]: true;
    }
    
    const ADDRESS = /^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{40}$/;
    const ZERO = /^0x0+$/;
    const HASH = /^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$/;
    const SCOPE_NAME = /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9:._-]{0,63}$/;
    
    function short(hex: string): string {
      return `${hex.slice(0, 6)}…${hex.slice(-4)}`;
    }
    
    export class IdentityRegistry {
      readonly #byKey = new Map<string, Identity>();
      readonly #issued = new WeakSet<Identity>();
      readonly #random: () => number;
      #ephemeralCounter = 0;
    
      constructor(random: () => number = Math.random) {
        this.#random = random;
      }
    
      /** The user's own account address. */
      account(address: string): Identity {
        if (!ADDRESS.test(address)) throw new AnonRpcError(`not an address: ${address}`, "POLICY");
        // 40 zeros occur in any ABI padding, so the zero address cannot be guarded; it is nobody's account.
        if (ZERO.test(address)) throw new AnonRpcError("the zero address is not an identity", "POLICY");
        const lower = address.toLowerCase();
        return this.#intern("account", `acct:${lower}`, `acct:${short(lower)}`, lower.slice(2));
      }
    
      /** A one-time stealth address. Each one is its own identity. */
      stealth(address: string): Identity {
        if (!ADDRESS.test(address) || ZERO.test(address)) throw new AnonRpcError(`not a stealth address: ${address}`, "POLICY");
        const lower = address.toLowerCase();
        return this.#intern("stealth", `stealth:${lower}`, `stealth:${short(lower)}`, lower.slice(2));
      }
    
      /** Waiting on or reading one transaction (`tx-wait:<hash>` in the notes). */
      tx(hash: string): Identity {
        if (!HASH.test(hash)) throw new AnonRpcError(`not a tx hash: ${hash}`, "POLICY");
        const lower = hash.toLowerCase();
        return this.#intern("tx", `tx:${lower}`, `tx:${short(lower)}`, lower.slice(2));
      }
    
      /**
       * A named scope for public data that names no user (head tracking, the
       * announcer scan, RAILGUN commitments). The name may not contain an address
       * or hash, so a public scope cannot be used to smuggle an identity in.
       */
      publicScope(name: string): Identity {
        if (!SCOPE_NAME.test(name) || /[0-9a-f]{40}/i.test(name)) {
          throw new AnonRpcError(`invalid public scope name: ${name}`, "POLICY");

      }
    
      /**
       * A throwaway identity used once (pre-warm, proof submission, bundler
       * calls). Never interned, so it can never be reused by accident.

      ephemeral(purpose: string): Identity {
        if (!SCOPE_NAME.test(purpose)) throw new AnonRpcError(`invalid purpose: ${purpose}`, "POLICY");
        const nonce = `${(++this.#ephemeralCounter).toString(36)}.${Math.floor(this.#random() * 2 ** 48).toString(36)}`;
        const id = this.#make("ephemeral", `eph:${purpose}:${nonce}`, `eph:${purpose}`, undefined);

      }
    
      isIssued(value: unknown): value is Identity {
        return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && this.#issued.has(value as Identity);
      }
    
      /**
       * Throws CROSS_IDENTITY if `text` (a URL or a request body) names the
       * subject of any other registered identity. Defence in depth: the API
       * already takes one identity per request, this also catches an address
       * that was pasted into params or calldata by mistake.

      assertOnlySubject(identity: Identity, text: string): void {
        if (!text) return;

        if (existing) return existing;
        const id = this.#make(kind, key, label, subject);
        this.#byKey.set(key, id);
        return id;
      }
    
      #make(kind: IdentityKind, key: string, label: string, subject: string | undefined): Identity {
        const id = Object.freeze({ kind, key, label, subject, [ISSUED]: true as const });
        this.#issued.add(id);
        return id;
      }
    }

    import { DEMO_GATEWAY, harnessWorkerFactory } from "@prawl/anon-rpc/harness-worker";
    
    export type TorPhase = "idle" | "booting" | "ready" | "failed";
    
    export interface TorStatus {

    function chooseGateway(): string {

      ownGateway: gateway !== DEMO_GATEWAY,
      chosenGateway: gateway !== buildGateway,
      worker: typeof worker === "string" ? worker : "custom",

    function onTrace(t: Trace): void {

      update({ traces });

    const host = new WorkerHost({
      factory: harnessWorkerFactory({ gateway, worker }),
      bootstrapLoader: () => loadOwnBootstrap(),
      onEvent,
    });
    
    /** HTTPS hosts the wallet may reach (besides JSON-RPC providers): RAILGUN's indexer, PPOI node, circuit artifacts, bundler. */
    export const HTTP_HOSTS = [
      "rail-squid.squids.live",
      "ppoi.fdi.network",
      "raw.githubusercontent.com",
      "ipfs-lb.com",
      "public.pimlico.io",

    export const anon = new AnonRpcClient({
      host,
      onTrace,
      prewarmChainId: SEPOLIA,
      httpHosts: HTTP_HOSTS,

    export const torStatus = {
      get: (): TorStatus => status,
      subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void {
        listeners.add(listener);
        return () => listeners.delete(listener);
      },
    };
    
    let started = false;
    export function startTor(): void {
      if (started) return;
      started = true;

    }

    const DIR_B_TO_A = 2;

      return fhevm.userDecryptEuint(FhevmType.euint64, handle as string, contract, user);
    }
    
    describe("VeilBatchSwap v3 (confidential orders)", function () {

      let batch: MockBatchSwap;
      let batchAddress: string, cAAddress: string, cBAddress: string;

        signers = { deployer: all[0], alice: all[1], bob: all[2], reserve: all[3] };
      });
    
      beforeEach(async function () {
        if (!fhevm.isMock) throw new Error("This suite runs on the FHEVM mock network only");
    
        tokenA = (await (await ethers.getContractFactory("ERC20Mock")).deploy("vUSD", "vUSD", 18)) as ERC20Mock;
        tokenB = (await (await ethers.getContractFactory("ERC20Mock")).deploy("vETH", "vETH", 18)) as ERC20Mock;
        cA = (await (await ethers.getContractFactory("VeilConfidentialWrapper")).deploy(await tokenA.getAddress())) as VeilConfidentialWrapper;
        cB = (await (await ethers.getContractFactory("VeilConfidentialWrapper")).deploy(await tokenB.getAddress())) as VeilConfidentialWrapper;
        cAAddress = await cA.getAddress();
        cBAddress = await cB.getAddress();
    
        const dummy = await signers.deployer.getAddress();

          currency0: BigInt(await tokenA.getAddress()) < BigInt(await tokenB.getAddress())
            ? await tokenA.getAddress() : await tokenB.getAddress(),
          currency1: BigInt(await tokenA.getAddress()) < BigInt(await tokenB.getAddress())
            ? await tokenB.getAddress() : await tokenA.getAddress(),
          fee: 3000,
          tickSpacing: 60,
          hooks: "0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000",
        };
        batch = (await (await ethers.getContractFactory("MockBatchSwap")).deploy(
          dummy, dummy, cAAddress, cBAddress, poolKey, 1, 2, await signers.deployer.getAddress(), await signers.reserve.getAddress(),
        )) as MockBatchSwap;
        batchAddress = await batch.getAddress();
    
        // 1:1 mock swap reserve.
        await (await tokenB.mint(await signers.reserve.getAddress(), WAD * 1_000_000_000_000n)).wait();
        await (await tokenB.connect(signers.reserve).approve(batchAddress, WAD * 1_000_000_000_000n)).wait();
        await (await tokenA.mint(await signers.reserve.getAddress(), WAD * 1_000_000_000_000n)).wait();
        await (await tokenA.connect(signers.reserve).approve(batchAddress, WAD * 1_000_000_000_000n)).wait();
      });
    
      async function wrap(user: HardhatEthersSigner, token: ERC20Mock, cToken: VeilConfidentialWrapper, amount: bigint) {
        const rate = await cToken.rate();
        await (await token.mint(user.address, amount * rate)).wait();
        await (await token.connect(user).approve(await cToken.getAddress(), amount * rate)).wait();
        await (await cToken.connect(user).wrap(user.address, amount * rate)).wait();
      }

      async function fund(user: HardhatEthersSigner, cToken: VeilConfidentialWrapper, amount: bigint) {
        const enc = await fhevm.createEncryptedInput(await cToken.getAddress(), user.address).add64(amount).encrypt();
        await (await cToken.connect(user)[
          "confidentialTransferAndCall(address,bytes32,bytes,bytes)"
        ](batchAddress, enc.handles[0], enc.inputProof, "0x00")).wait();
      }
