      async function order(user: HardhatEthersSigner, direction: number, amount: bigint) {
        const enc = await fhevm.createEncryptedInput(batchAddress, user.address).add64(amount).add8(direction).encrypt();
        await (await batch.connect(user).submitOrder(enc.handles[0], enc.inputProof, enc.handles[1], enc.inputProof)).wait();
      }
    
      async function fundedBalance(user: HardhatEthersSigner, asset: number): Promise<bigint> {
        const handle = await batch.fundedBalance(user.address, asset);
        if (handle === 0n) return 0n;
        return decryptUser(handle, batchAddress, user);
      }
    
      /** Close, decrypt ONLY (net, surplus), prepare, execute, credit both. */
      async function settleAndCredit() {
        const roundId = await batch.currentRound(); // the round being closed
        await (await batch.closeRound()).wait();
        const round = await batch.rounds(roundId);
        const dec = await fhevm.publicDecrypt([round.netEnc, round.surplusEnc]);
        const net = dec.clearValues[round.netEnc];
        const surplus = dec.clearValues[round.surplusEnc];
        await (await batch.prepareSettle(roundId, dec.abiEncodedClearValues, dec.decryptionProof)).wait();
        if (net > 0n) {
          const prepared = await batch.rounds(roundId);
          const dec2 = await fhevm.publicDecrypt([prepared.unwrapHandle]);
          const cleartext = dec2.clearValues[prepared.unwrapHandle];
          await (await batch.executeSettle(roundId, cleartext, dec2.decryptionProof)).wait();
        } else {
          await (await batch.executeSettle(roundId, 0n, "0x")).wait();
        }
        void surplus;
        for (const u of [signers.alice, signers.bob]) {
          await (await batch.creditOrder(roundId, u.address)).wait();
        }
        return roundId;
      }
    
      it("funding credits an encrypted balance the user can decrypt", async function () {
        await wrap(signers.alice, tokenA, cA, 10n * 10n ** 6n);
        await fund(signers.alice, cA, 10n * 10n ** 6n);
        expect(await fundedBalance(signers.alice, 0)).to.equal(10n * 10n ** 6n);
        expect(await fundedBalance(signers.alice, 1)).to.equal(0n);
      });

    /** A measured gas outside this band is treated as noise (a note arrived or left mid-operation) and ignored. */

    export function maxPrivateAmountWei(kind: "send" | "unshield", available: bigint): bigint {
      const rest = available - privateGasEstimateWei();

      const status = (s: string) => updateAction(myEpoch, { kind: actionKind, status: s });
      try {
        const before = wethTotal();
        status("Proving in your browser… (this takes a while)");
        const oneTimeKey = generatePrivateKey();
        const sender = privateKeyToAddress(oneTimeKey);
        railgun().useAccount(sender);
        const prepared = await run(oneTimeKey);
        status("Sending to the bundler over Tor…");
        const tx = await sendUserOp(anon, state.chainId, sender, prepared.signed, prepared.userOpHash, status);
        await remember(opLog, myEpoch, kind, amountWei, to, [tx]);
        if (!inner || tx.status !== "success") updateAction(myEpoch, { kind: actionKind, status: tx.status === "success" ? "Done." : "The operation reverted", done: true, txs: [tx] });
        await sync();
        // What the paymaster took: everything that left the balance beyond the amount and fee.
        const gas = before - wethTotal() - amountWei - privateFeeWei(kind, amountWei);
        if (tx.status === "success" && gas >= GAS_MEASURE_MIN && gas <= GAS_MEASURE_MAX && myEpoch === epoch) update({ privateGasWei: gas });
        return tx.status === "success";
      } catch (e) {
        updateAction(myEpoch, { ...failedAction(kind === "send" ? "Send failed" : "Unshield failed", e, explain(kind, e)), kind: actionKind });
        if (e instanceof UnconfirmedError) void sync();
        return false;
      }
    }
    
    /** Send private ETH (WETH notes) to another 0zk address. */
    export function privateSendEth(recipient: string, amountWei: bigint): Promise<boolean> {
      const weth = state.wrappedBaseToken as Hex | undefined;
      if (!weth) throw new Error("wallet is not open");
      return privateOp("send", amountWei, recipient, (accountPrivateKey) =>
        railgun().call("prepareTransfer", { recipient, token: weth, amount: amountWei.toString(), accountPrivateKey, bundlerUrl: BUNDLER_URL }),
      );
    }
    
    /**
     * Withdraw private ETH to a public address; the 0.25% RAILGUN fee is added on top.
     * As native ETH (default): WETH is unshielded to the one-time account, unwrapped
     * there, and the ETH forwarded to the recipient in the same UserOperation.
     * With `asWeth`, the recipient gets WETH directly (cheaper).
     */
    export function unshieldEth(recipient: Hex, amountWei: bigint, opts: { asWeth?: boolean; inner?: { kind: string } } = {}): Promise<boolean> {
      const weth = state.wrappedBaseToken as Hex | undefined;
      if (!weth) throw new Error("wallet is not open");
      return privateOp("unshield", amountWei, recipient, (accountPrivateKey) =>
        railgun().call(
          "prepareUnshield",
          opts.asWeth
            ? { recipient, token: weth, amount: amountWei.toString(), accountPrivateKey, bundlerUrl: BUNDLER_URL }
            : {
                recipient: privateKeyToAddress(accountPrivateKey),
                token: weth,
                amount: amountWei.toString(),
                unwrapNative: true,
                tailCalls: [{ target: recipient, value: amountWei.toString(), data: "0x" }],
                accountPrivateKey,
                bundlerUrl: BUNDLER_URL,
              },
        ),
        opts.inner,
      );
    }
    
    /** Forget a finished action's result (e.g. when a new offline payload is opened). A running action stays. */
    export function clearFinishedAction(): void {
      if (state.action?.done) update({ action: undefined });
    }
    
    /**
     * Offline cash, load: deposit a phone-minted coin into the escrow from this
     * wallet's public account (the coin commits to it as depositor). Checked and
     * simulated before signing; sent over the account's own Tor circuit.
     */
    export async function offlineLoad(p: LoadPayload): Promise<void> {
    /**
     * Key derivation. One 32-byte root secret per wallet (from a passkey's PRF
     * output, see passkey.ts) is expanded with HKDF-SHA256 into independent keys,
     * each bound to its purpose by a distinct `info` label. Knowing one derived key
     * reveals nothing about the root or the others.
     *
     * Derived keys are returned as bytes/hex only to hand straight to the code that
     * uses them (the RAILGUN wasm, the EVM signer). Never log, persist or send them.
     */
    import { bytesToHex, hexToBigInt, type Hex } from "viem";
    
    const SALT = new TextEncoder().encode("prawl/v1");
    const SECP256K1_N = 0xfffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffebaaedce6af48a03bbfd25e8cd0364141n;
    
    export const LABELS = {
      railgunSpending: "prawl/v1/railgun/spending",
      railgunViewing: "prawl/v1/railgun/viewing",
      evmAccount: "prawl/v1/evm/account",
      storage: "prawl/v1/storage/aes-gcm",
    } as const;
    
    export interface WalletKeys {
      /** RAILGUN spending key (32 bytes, 0x-hex). */
      railgunSpending: Hex;
      /** RAILGUN viewing key (32 bytes, 0x-hex). */
      railgunViewing: Hex;
      /** secp256k1 private key of the wallet's EVM account (signs UserOperations). */
      evmAccount: Hex;
      /** Non-extractable AES-GCM key for the local vault. */
      storage: CryptoKey;
    }
    
    async function hkdfBits(root: CryptoKey, info: string, bits = 256): Promise<Uint8Array> {
      const out = await crypto.subtle.deriveBits(
        { name: "HKDF", hash: "SHA-256", salt: SALT, info: new TextEncoder().encode(info) },
        root,
        bits,
      );
      return new Uint8Array(out);
    }
    
    async function importRoot(root: Uint8Array): Promise<CryptoKey> {
      if (root.byteLength !== 32) throw new Error("root secret must be 32 bytes");
      return crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", root as BufferSource, "HKDF", false, ["deriveBits", "deriveKey"]);
    }
    
    /** A valid secp256k1 scalar (1..n-1): re-derive with a counter until in range. */
    async function evmKey(root: CryptoKey): Promise<Hex> {
      for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) {
        const label = i === 0 ? LABELS.evmAccount : `${LABELS.evmAccount}/${i}`;
        const hex = bytesToHex(await hkdfBits(root, label));
        const k = hexToBigInt(hex);
        if (k > 0n && k < SECP256K1_N) return hex;
      }
      throw new Error("could not derive an EVM key");
    }
    
    export async function deriveWalletKeys(rootSecret: Uint8Array): Promise<WalletKeys> {
      const root = await importRoot(rootSecret);
      const [railgunSpending, railgunViewing, evmAccount, storage] = await Promise.all([
        hkdfBits(root, LABELS.railgunSpending).then(bytesToHex),
        hkdfBits(root, LABELS.railgunViewing).then(bytesToHex),
        evmKey(root),
        crypto.subtle.deriveKey(
          { name: "HKDF", hash: "SHA-256", salt: SALT, info: new TextEncoder().encode(LABELS.storage) },
          root,
          { name: "AES-GCM", length: 256 },
          false,
          ["encrypt", "decrypt"],
        ),
      ]);
      return { railgunSpending, railgunViewing, evmAccount, storage };
    }

    contract VeilConfidentialWrapper is ZamaEthereumConfig, ERC7984ERC20Wrapper {
        constructor(address assetAddress)
            ERC7984(
                string.concat("Veil c", IERC20Metadata(assetAddress).name()),

## Assistant

The prototype has a material accounting issue for a production integration: its payout multiplies two 64-bit values before division, so intermediate overflow can corrupt a user’s credit. Its fixed 1:1 matching also cannot safely price WETH against a general token. I’m testing the current implementation and will use those results to guide the contract changes.

    /**

     * so it is sealed with the passkey-derived storage key, like the RAILGUN

    import { open, seal, type Sealed } from "@prawl/keys";
    
    export type ActivityKind = "shield" | "send" | "unshield" | "public-send" | "offline-load" | "offline-redeem";
    
    export interface Activity {
      /** Unix ms. */
      at: number;
      kind: ActivityKind;
      /** Wei, decimal string. */
      amount: string;
      /** Recipient (0zk or 0x) where there is one. */
      to?: string;
      txHash: string;
      ok: boolean;
    }
    
    export interface KvStore {
      get<T>(key: string): Promise<T | undefined>;
      set(key: string, value: unknown): Promise<unknown>;
    }
    
    /** Most entries kept; the oldest are dropped first. */
    export const MAX_ACTIVITY = 200;
    
    export class ActivityLog {
      #items: Activity[] = [];
      #write: Promise<unknown> = Promise.resolve();
    
      constructor(
        private readonly store: KvStore,
        private readonly key: CryptoKey,
        private readonly name: string,
      ) {}
    
      /** Load and open the sealed history. A record this key cannot open (another wallet, corrupt) reads as empty. */
      async load(): Promise<Activity[]> {
        const sealed = await this.store.get<Sealed>(this.name);
        if (!sealed) return (this.#items = []);
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(await open(this.key, this.name, sealed)) as unknown;
          this.#items = Array.isArray(parsed) ? (parsed as Activity[]).filter(isActivity) : [];
        } catch {
          this.#items = [];
        }
        return this.list();
      }
    
      /** Newest first. */
      list(): Activity[] {
        return [...this.#items].sort((a, b) => b.at - a.at);
      }
    
      /** Add an entry and save; writes are serialized so none is lost. */
      async add(entry: Activity): Promise<Activity[]> {
        this.#items = [...this.#items, entry].sort((a, b) => a.at - b.at).slice(-MAX_ACTIVITY);
        const snapshot = JSON.stringify(this.#items);
        this.#write = this.#write.then(async () => this.store.set(this.name, await seal(this.key, this.name, snapshot)));
        await this.#write;
        return this.list();
      }
    }

    /**

     * which allows users to transfer `ERC1363` tokens directly to the wrapper with a callback to wrap the tokens.

     * WARNING: Minting assumes the full amount of the underlying token transfer has been received, hence some non-standard
     * tokens such as fee-on-transfer or other deflationary-type tokens are not supported by this wrapper.
     */
    abstract contract ERC7984ERC20Wrapper is ERC7984, IERC1363Receiver {

        uint256 private immutable _rate;

        event UnwrapFinalized(address indexed receiver, euint64 encryptedAmount, uint64 cleartextAmount);

        constructor(IERC20 underlying_) {
            _underlying = underlying_;
    
            uint8 tokenDecimals = _tryGetAssetDecimals(underlying_);
            uint8 maxDecimals = _maxDecimals();
            if (tokenDecimals > maxDecimals) {
                _decimals = maxDecimals;
                _rate = 10 ** (tokenDecimals - maxDecimals);
            } else {
                _decimals = tokenDecimals;
                _rate = 1;
            }
        }

        function onTransferReceived(
            address /*operator*/,
            address from,
            uint256 amount,
            bytes calldata data
        ) public virtual returns (bytes4) {
            // check caller is the token contract
            require(address(underlying()) == msg.sender, ERC7984UnauthorizedCaller(msg.sender));
    
            // mint confidential token
            address to = data.length < 20 ? from : address(bytes20(data));
            _mint(to, FHE.asEuint64(SafeCast.toUint64(amount / rate())));
    
            // transfer excess back to the sender
            uint256 excess = amount % rate();
            if (excess > 0) SafeERC20.safeTransfer(underlying(), from, excess);

        }
    
        /**

        function wrap(address to, uint256 amount) public virtual {
            // take ownership of the tokens
            SafeERC20.safeTransferFrom(underlying(), msg.sender, address(this), amount - (amount % rate()));

      // ------------------------------------------------------------------- HTTP
    
      /** HTTP GET/POST to an allowlisted host through the worker (RAILGUN, beacon, ASP). */
      async http(identity: Identity, req: HttpRequest): Promise<HttpResult> {
        let url: URL;
        try {
          url = new URL(req.url);
        } catch {
          throw new AnonRpcError(`invalid URL ${req.url}`, "POLICY");
        }
        if (url.protocol !== "https:") throw new AnonRpcError("https only", "POLICY");
        if (!this.#httpHosts.has(url.host.toLowerCase())) {
          throw new AnonRpcError(`host ${url.host} is not on the allowlist`, "POLICY");
        }
        const method = req.method ?? "GET";
        this.#check(identity, `${url.href} ${req.body ?? ""}`);
        const raw = await this.#send({
          kind: req.kind === "write" ? "write" : method === "GET" ? "get" : "read",
          identity,
          label: `http:${method}`,
          targets: [{ id: url.host, url: url.href }],
          method,
          headers: req.headers ?? {},
          ...(req.body !== undefined ? { body: req.body } : {}),
          deadlines: { ...this.#deadlines, ...req.deadlines },
          ...(req.signal ? { signal: req.signal } : {}),
          passHttpErrors: req.passHttpErrors ?? false,
          // A caller that raised the headers deadline expects a slow answer (Subsquid pages).
          ...((req.deadlines?.headersMs ?? 0) > this.#deadlines.headersMs ? { reusedHeadersTimeoutMs: 0 } : {}),
        });
        return { status: raw.status, statusText: raw.statusText, headers: raw.headers, body: raw.body, url: url.href };
      }

      /** Latest block number, on a neutral public scope, shared by all callers for `headTtlMs`. */
      async head(chainId: number): Promise<bigint> {
        const cached = this.#heads.get(chainId);
        if (cached && this.#now() - cached.at < this.#headTtlMs) return cached.value;
        const pending = this.#headInflight.get(chainId);
        if (pending) return pending;
        const p = (async () => {
          const scope = this.identities.publicScope(`head:${chainId}`);
          this.#check(scope, "[]");

          const value = BigInt(hex);

    export class RailgunController {

        this.#progress.add(fn);

      it("two-sided round: matched 1:1, surplus share via public net, credited confidentially", async function () {
        await wrap(signers.alice, tokenA, cA, 5n * 10n ** 6n);
        await fund(signers.alice, cA, 5n * 10n ** 6n);
        await wrap(signers.bob, tokenB, cB, 3n * 10n ** 6n);
        await fund(signers.bob, cB, 3n * 10n ** 6n);
        await order(signers.alice, DIR_A_TO_B, 5n * 10n ** 6n);
        await order(signers.bob, DIR_B_TO_A, 3n * 10n ** 6n);
    
        await settleAndCredit();
    
        // 1:1 mock swap: alice (surplus, sold 5) gets 3 matched + 2 residual → 5 cB.
        // bob (deficit, sold 3) gets 3 matched → 3 cA.
        expect(await fundedBalance(signers.alice, 1)).to.equal(5n * 10n ** 6n);
        expect(await fundedBalance(signers.alice, 0)).to.equal(0n);
        expect(await fundedBalance(signers.bob, 0)).to.equal(3n * 10n ** 6n);
        expect(await fundedBalance(signers.bob, 1)).to.equal(0n);
      });
    
      it("fully matched round pays both sides 1:1 with no swap leg", async function () {
        await wrap(signers.alice, tokenA, cA, 4n * 10n ** 6n);
        await fund(signers.alice, cA, 4n * 10n ** 6n);
        await wrap(signers.bob, tokenB, cB, 4n * 10n ** 6n);
        await fund(signers.bob, cB, 4n * 10n ** 6n);
        await order(signers.alice, DIR_A_TO_B, 4n * 10n ** 6n);
        await order(signers.bob, DIR_B_TO_A, 4n * 10n ** 6n);
        await settleAndCredit();
        expect(await fundedBalance(signers.alice, 1)).to.equal(4n * 10n ** 6n);
        expect(await fundedBalance(signers.bob, 0)).to.equal(4n * 10n ** 6n);
      });
    
      it("creditOrder is permissionless but single-credit", async function () {
        await wrap(signers.alice, tokenA, cA, 4n * 10n ** 6n);
        await fund(signers.alice, cA, 4n * 10n ** 6n);
        await wrap(signers.bob, tokenB, cB, 4n * 10n ** 6n);
        await fund(signers.bob, cB, 4n * 10n ** 6n);
        await order(signers.alice, DIR_A_TO_B, 4n * 10n ** 6n);
        await order(signers.bob, DIR_B_TO_A, 4n * 10n ** 6n);
        const roundId = await settleAndCredit();
        await expect(batch.creditOrder(roundId, signers.alice.address)).to.be.revertedWithCustomError(batch, "AlreadyCredited");
        expect(await fundedBalance(signers.alice, 1)).to.equal(4n * 10n ** 6n);
      });
    
      it("exit unwraps the funded balance to plain tokens (public leg by design)", async function () {
        await wrap(signers.alice, tokenA, cA, 6n * 10n ** 6n);
        await fund(signers.alice, cA, 6n * 10n ** 6n);
        const rate = await cA.rate();
        const before = await tokenA.balanceOf(signers.alice.address);
        const enc = await fhevm.createEncryptedInput(batchAddress, signers.alice.address).add64(2n * 10n ** 6n).encrypt();
        await (await batch.connect(signers.alice).exit(0, enc.handles[0], enc.inputProof)).wait();
        // async unwrap: finalize the pending request
        const wAbi = ["event UnwrapRequested(address indexed receiver, bytes32 amount)"];
        const w = new ethers.Contract(cAAddress, wAbi, signers.alice);
        const reqs = await w.queryFilter(w.filters.UnwrapRequested());
        expect(reqs.length).to.equal(1);
        const handle = (reqs[0].args as unknown as { amount: string }).amount;
        const dec = await fhevm.publicDecrypt([handle]);
        await (await cA.finalizeUnwrap(handle, dec.clearValues[handle], dec.decryptionProof)).wait();
        expect(await tokenA.balanceOf(signers.alice.address)).to.equal(before + 2n * 10n ** 6n * rate);
        expect(await fundedBalance(signers.alice, 0)).to.equal(4n * 10n ** 6n);
      });
    
      it("one order per user per round", async function () {
        await wrap(signers.alice, tokenA, cA, 5n * 10n ** 6n);
        await fund(signers.alice, cA, 5n * 10n ** 6n);
        await order(signers.alice, DIR_A_TO_B, 1n * 10n ** 6n);
        await expect(order(signers.alice, DIR_A_TO_B, 1n * 10n ** 6n)).to.be.revertedWithCustomError(batch, "OrderAlreadySubmitted");
      });
    });

## Assistant

The existing FHE test suite passes, and I reproduced the large-order arithmetic boundary in a new Prawl protocol workspace. I’ve widened the payout calculation and added a test that would lose funds with the original 64-bit intermediate. The prototype still has parity pricing, so I’m keeping it local-only while the pricing and wallet integration are developed.

    /** Messages between the wallet page and the RAILGUN worker. */
    import type { Balance, Call, Hex, Note, PoiReport, PreparedUserOp, SyncProgress } from "@prawl/railgun";
    
    export interface Deployment {
      chainId: number;
      deploymentBlock: string;
      smartWallet: string;
      wrappedBaseToken: string;
      unshieldFeeBps: number;
      subsquidEndpoint: string;
      poiEndpoint: string;
    }
    
    export interface OpenArgs {
      chainId: number;
      rpcUrl: string;
      spendingKey: Hex;
      viewingKey: Hex;
      /** Non-extractable AES-GCM key; the worker seals checkpoints with it. */
      storageKey: CryptoKey;
      /** Record name the sealed checkpoint is stored under. */
      checkpointName: string;
      /**
       * True only for a wallet created just now: it cannot own notes from before
       * it existed, so with no saved checkpoint it may start from our public pool
       * snapshot instead of replaying all history. Never for a restored wallet.
       */
      fresh: boolean;
      /**
       * When the wallet was created (unix seconds, from the passkey). A restore
       * starts from the pool snapshot and replays only from a block before this.
       */
      bornAt?: number;
      /** Drop the saved checkpoint and scan all history again. */
      discardCheckpoint?: boolean;
    }
    
    export interface Ops {
      deployment(chainId: number): Deployment;
      open(args: OpenArgs): { address: string; syncedBlock: string; restored: boolean; snapshotBlock?: string; fullScan: boolean; replayFrom?: string };
      sync(args: { head: string; windowBlocks?: string }): { syncedBlock: string };
      refreshPoi(): void;
      /** Number of our own operations whose PPOI proofs are not submitted yet. */
      poiPendingCount(): number;
      /** Prove and submit queued PPOI proofs (after a private send or unshield lands). */
      processPendingPoi(): PoiReport;
      balances(): Balance[];
      notes(): Note[];
      buildShield(args: { recipient: string; items: Array<{ token: "native" | Hex; amount: string }> }): Call[];
      prepareTransfer(args: { recipient: string; token: Hex; amount: string; memo?: string; accountPrivateKey: Hex; bundlerUrl: string }): PreparedUserOp;
      prepareUnshield(args: {
        recipient: Hex;
        token: Hex;
        amount: string;
        unwrapNative?: boolean;
        tailCalls?: Array<{ target: Hex; value: string; data: Hex }>;
        accountPrivateKey: Hex;
        bundlerUrl: string;
      }): PreparedUserOp;
      close(): void;
    }
    
    export type OpName = keyof Ops;
    
    export type ToWorker =
      | { t: "call"; id: number; op: OpName; args: unknown }
      | { t: "fetch-result"; id: number; ok: true; status: number; statusText: string; headers: [string, string][]; body: ArrayBuffer | null; url: string }
      | { t: "fetch-result"; id: number; ok: false; error: string };
    
    export type FromWorker =
      | { t: "result"; id: number; ok: true; value: unknown }
      | { t: "result"; id: number; ok: false; error: string }
      | { t: "progress"; progress: { from: string; to: string; synced: string; head: string; ms: number } }
      | { t: "fetch"; id: number; url: string; method: string; headers: [string, string][]; body: ArrayBuffer | null };
    
    export type ProgressMessage = Extract<FromWorker, { t: "progress" }>["progress"];
    export type { PoiReport, SyncProgress };
    /**
     * All of RAILGUN's network I/O, over Anon-RPC.
     *
     * kohaku-railgun-wasm calls the global `fetch` (reqwest) for four things:
     *  - its JSON-RPC provider, given as one URL. We give it a virtual URL
     *    (https://<rpcHost>/<chainId>) and answer it here by sending each call
     *    as an Anon-RPC read: on the wallet's own account identity when the call
     *    names the account address, else on a public RAILGUN scope (commitment
     *    logs, contract reads that name nobody). Writes are refused: Prawl sends
     *    transactions and UserOperations itself.
     *  - Subsquid history, the PPOI node, and circuit artifacts, and
     *  - the bundler, all handled by @prawl/anon-rpc's createRailgunFetch.
     * Anything else fails like a network error and never reaches the real fetch.
     */
    import { AnonRpcError, createRailgunFetch, type AnonRpcClient, type Identity } from "@prawl/anon-rpc";
    
    export const DEFAULT_RPC_HOST = "rpc.railgun.prawl.invalid";
    export const ARTIFACT_HOSTS = ["raw.githubusercontent.com", "ipfs-lb.com"];
    
    export interface TransportOptions {
      client: AnonRpcClient;
      chainId: number;
      subsquidEndpoint: string;
      poiEndpoint: string;
      /** The wallet's EVM account (signs UserOperations), if it has one. */
      accountAddress?: string;
      bundlerHosts?: readonly string[];
      artifactHosts?: readonly string[];
      rpcHost?: string;
    }
    
    export interface RailgunTransport {
      fetch: typeof fetch;
      /** Pass to RailgunWallet.open as rpcUrl. */
      rpcUrl: string;
      /**
       * Register another account of this wallet (e.g. a one-time UserOperation
       * sender). RPC calls that name it then go on its own identity, not on the
       * shared public RAILGUN scope.
       */
      addAccount(address: string): void;
    }
    
    interface JsonRpcCall {
      jsonrpc?: string;
      id?: unknown;
      method?: unknown;
      params?: unknown;
    }
    
    function hostOf(url: string): string {
      return new URL(url).host.toLowerCase();
    }
    
    function jsonResponse(body: unknown, url: string): Response {
      const res = new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status: 200, headers: { "content-type": "application/json" } });
      // reqwest's wasm client rejects a Response whose url is empty.
      Object.defineProperty(res, "url", { value: url });
      return res;
    }
    
    function toRpcError(e: unknown): { code: number; message: string; data?: unknown } {
      if (e instanceof AnonRpcError) {
        if (e.code === "RPC_ERROR") return { code: e.rpcCode ?? -32000, message: e.message, ...(e.rpcData !== undefined ? { data: e.rpcData } : {}) };
        return { code: -32603, message: `${e.code}: ${e.message}` };
      }
      return { code: -32603, message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) };
    }
    
    export function createRailgunTransport(opts: TransportOptions): RailgunTransport {
      const rpcHost = (opts.rpcHost ?? DEFAULT_RPC_HOST).toLowerCase();
      const rpcUrl = `https://${rpcHost}/${opts.chainId}`;
      const client = opts.client;
      const publicId = client.identities.publicScope(`railgun-${opts.chainId}`);
      const accounts = new Map<string, Identity>();
      const addAccount = (address: string) => {
        const id = client.identities.account(address);
        accounts.set(address.toLowerCase().replace(/^0x/, ""), id);
      };
      if (opts.accountAddress) addAccount(opts.accountAddress);
    
      /** The registered account a request names, if any (it must then travel on that account's identity). */
      const accountIn = (text: string): Identity | undefined => {
        const lower = text.toLowerCase();
        for (const [hex, id] of accounts) if (lower.includes(hex)) return id;
        return undefined;
      };
      const identityFor = (call: JsonRpcCall): Identity => accountIn(JSON.stringify(call.params ?? [])) ?? publicId;
    
      const answer = async (call: JsonRpcCall): Promise<unknown> => {
        const id = call.id ?? null;
        if (typeof call.method !== "string") return { jsonrpc: "2.0", id, error: { code: -32600, message: "invalid request" } };
        const params = Array.isArray(call.params) ? call.params : [];
        try {
          const result = await client.read(identityFor(call), opts.chainId, call.method, params);
          return { jsonrpc: "2.0", id, result };
        } catch (e) {
          return { jsonrpc: "2.0", id, error: toRpcError(e) };
        }
      };

    Add: /home/sahilkaushik/study_material/blockchain/prawl/private-swap/README.md
    # Prawl confidential swap protocol (development)
    
    This directory is the contract development workspace for Prawl's confidential
    swap. It starts from the ETHGlobal encrypted vault and batching prototype.
    It is **not deployable for production or for a general WETH/token pair**:
    the inherited matcher assumes a fixed 1:1 price, lacks per-order execution
    bounds and does not provide adequate settlement recovery. The Hardhat
    configuration deliberately defines only a local network.
    
    Run `npm ci` and `npm test` in this directory. The FHEVM mock tests exercise
    the encrypted funding, order, settlement and withdrawal lifecycle. They do
    not verify the real FHE gateway or a real Uniswap pool.

    /** Network boundary for the FHE SDK. No request here uses the browser network. */
    import type { AnonRpcClient } from "@prawl/anon-rpc";
    
    export const FHE_SEPOLIA_CHAIN_ID = 11155111;
    const RELAYER = "relayer.testnet.zama.org";
    const KEY_STORE = "zama-mpc-testnet-public-efd88e2b.s3.eu-west-1.amazonaws.com";
    const RPC_METHODS = new Set(["eth_chainId", "eth_blockNumber", "eth_call", "eth_getCode"]);
    
    /** Both gateway requests and SDK contract reads use this wallet's isolated circuit. */
    export function createFheTransport(client: AnonRpcClient, account: string, chainId: number) {
      if (chainId !== FHE_SEPOLIA_CHAIN_ID) throw new Error("FHE swap is not configured for this chain");
      const identity = client.identities.account(account);
    
      return {
        async rpc(method: string, params: unknown[] = []): Promise<unknown> {
          if (!RPC_METHODS.has(method)) throw new Error(`FHE RPC method not allowed: ${method}`);
          return client.read(identity, chainId, method, params);
        },
    
        async fetch(url: string, method: string, headers: [string, string][], body: ArrayBuffer | null): Promise<Response> {
          const target = new URL(url);
          const verb = method.toUpperCase();
          if (target.protocol !== "https:" || target.username || target.password || target.port || target.hash) {
            throw new Error("FHE endpoint is not allowed");
          }
          if (target.hostname !== RELAYER && target.hostname !== KEY_STORE) throw new Error("FHE endpoint is not allowed");
          if (verb !== "GET" && verb !== "POST") throw new Error("FHE HTTP method is not allowed");
          if (target.hostname === KEY_STORE && verb !== "GET") throw new Error("FHE key store is read only");
          if (verb === "GET" && body) throw new Error("FHE GET request cannot have a body");
          const result = await client.http(identity, {
            url: target.href,
            method: verb,
            headers: Object.fromEntries(headers),
            ...(body ? { body: new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true }).decode(body) } : {}),
            ...(verb === "POST" ? { kind: "write" as const } : {}),
            passHttpErrors: true,
          });
          const response = new Response(result.body as BodyInit, { status: result.status, statusText: result.statusText, headers: result.headers });
          Object.defineProperty(response, "url", { value: result.url });
          return response;
        },
      };
    }

    import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
    import type { AnonRpcClient } from "@prawl/anon-rpc";
    import { createFheTransport, FHE_SEPOLIA_CHAIN_ID } from "../src/swap/transport";
    
    const account = "0x1111111111111111111111111111111111111111";
    const relayer = "https://relayer.testnet.zama.org/v2/user-decrypt";
    
    function client() {
      const identity = { key: "account-circuit" };
      const fake = {
        identities: { account: vi.fn(() => identity) },
        read: vi.fn(async () => "0x1234"),
        http: vi.fn(async (_id: unknown, req: { url: string }) => ({
          status: 200, statusText: "OK", headers: new Headers({ "content-type": "application/json" }),
          body: new TextEncoder().encode('{"ok":true}'), url: req.url,
        })),
      };
      return { fake, identity, transport: createFheTransport(fake as unknown as AnonRpcClient, account, FHE_SEPOLIA_CHAIN_ID) };
    }
    
    describe("FHE transport", () => {
      it("sends relayer writes and contract reads through the account's Tor identity", async () => {
        const { fake, identity, transport } = client();
        const body = new TextEncoder().encode('{"handle":"0x01"}').buffer;
        const res = await transport.fetch(relayer, "POST", [["content-type", "application/json"]], body);
        expect(await res.json()).toEqual({ ok: true });
        expect(fake.http).toHaveBeenCalledWith(identity, expect.objectContaining({ url: relayer, method: "POST", kind: "write", body: '{"handle":"0x01"}' }));
        expect(await transport.rpc("eth_call", [{ to: account }, "latest"])).toBe("0x1234");
        expect(fake.read).toHaveBeenCalledWith(identity, FHE_SEPOLIA_CHAIN_ID, "eth_call", [{ to: account }, "latest"]);
      });
    
      it("rejects untrusted endpoints and methods before sending anything", async () => {
        const { fake, transport } = client();
        for (const [url, method] of [
          ["https://attacker.example/key", "GET"],
          ["http://relayer.testnet.zama.org/key", "GET"],
          ["https://relayer.testnet.zama.org.evil.example/key", "GET"],
          ["https://zama-mpc-testnet-public-efd88e2b.s3.eu-west-1.amazonaws.com/key", "POST"],
          [relayer, "DELETE"],

## Assistant

The wallet also needs to keep FHE traffic within its existing Tor boundary. I’ve added a restricted transport for the Sepolia FHE gateway and key store, with tests that reject unexpected hosts and transaction methods. Next I’m wiring the encryption runtime through a dedicated worker so SDK network calls cannot silently fall back to the browser’s direct connection.

    /// <reference lib="webworker" />
    import { bytesToHex, getAddress } from "viem";
    import type { FhevmInstance } from "@zama-fhe/relayer-sdk/web";
    import type { FromFheWorker, ToFheWorker, WorkerCall } from "./fhe-protocol";
    
    declare const self: DedicatedWorkerGlobalScope;
    const localFetch = self.fetch.bind(self);
    const send = (message: FromFheWorker, transfer: Transferable[] = []) => self.postMessage(message, transfer);
    let sequence = 0;
    const pending = new Map<number, { resolve: (value: any) => void; reject: (error: Error) => void }>();
    
    // SDK wasm is served with the app. All other fetches are bridged to the
    // page's allowlisted Anon-RPC transport; never fall back to direct fetch.
    self.fetch = (async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
      const request = input instanceof Request ? input : new Request(input, init);
      const url = new URL(request.url);
      if (url.origin === self.location.origin) return localFetch(request);
      const body = request.method === "GET" || request.method === "HEAD" ? null : await request.arrayBuffer();
      const headers: [string, string][] = [];
      request.headers.forEach((value, key) => headers.push([key, value]));
      const id = ++sequence;
      return new Promise<Response>((resolve, reject) => {
        if (request.signal.aborted) { reject(new DOMException("Aborted", "AbortError")); return; }
        pending.set(id, { resolve, reject });
        request.signal.addEventListener("abort", () => {
          if (pending.delete(id)) reject(new DOMException("Aborted", "AbortError"));
        }, { once: true });
        send({ type: "fetch", id, url: url.href, method: request.method, headers, body }, body ? [body] : []);
      });
    }) as typeof fetch;
    
    function rpc(method: string, params: unknown[] = []): Promise<unknown> {
      const id = ++sequence;
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        pending.set(id, { resolve, reject });
        send({ type: "rpc", id, method, params });
      });
    }
    
    let instance: FhevmInstance | undefined;
    let owner: string | undefined;
    
    async function run(call: WorkerCall): Promise<unknown> {
      if (call.op === "init") {
        if (instance) throw new Error("FHE worker already initialized");
        if (call.chainId !== 11155111) throw new Error("Unsupported FHE chain");
        owner = getAddress(call.account);
        const sdk = await import("@zama-fhe/relayer-sdk/web");
        await sdk.initSDK({ thread: 1 });
        instance = await sdk.createInstance({
          ...sdk.SepoliaConfig,
          network: { request: ({ method, params }: { method: string; params?: unknown[] }) => rpc(method, params) },
          batchRpcCalls: false,
        });
        return true;
      }
      if (!instance || !owner) throw new Error("FHE worker is not initialized");
      const amount = BigInt(call.amount);
      if (amount <= 0n || amount > (1n << 64n) - 1n) throw new Error("Amount is outside the confidential token range");
      if (call.direction !== undefined && call.direction !== 1 && call.direction !== 2) throw new Error("Invalid swap direction");
      const input = instance.createEncryptedInput(getAddress(call.contract), owner).add64(amount);
      if (call.direction !== undefined) input.add8(call.direction);
      const encrypted = await input.encrypt();
      return { handles: encrypted.handles.map(bytesToHex), proof: bytesToHex(encrypted.inputProof) };
    }
    
    self.onmessage = (event: MessageEvent<ToFheWorker>) => {
      const message = event.data;
      if (message.type === "call") {
        void run(message.call).then(
          (value) => send({ type: "result", id: message.id, ok: true, value }),
          (error: unknown) => send({ type: "result", id: message.id, ok: false, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) }),
        );
        return;
      }
      const request = pending.get(message.id);
      if (!request) return;
      pending.delete(message.id);
      if (!message.ok) { request.reject(new Error(message.error)); return; }
      if (message.type === "rpc-result") { request.resolve(message.value); return; }
      const response = new Response(message.body, { status: message.status, statusText: message.statusText, headers: message.headers });
      Object.defineProperty(response, "url", { value: message.url });
      request.resolve(response);
    };

    import type { Hex } from "viem";
    import { anon } from "../lib/anon";
    import { createFheTransport } from "./transport";
    import type { EncryptedInput, FromFheWorker, ToFheWorker, WorkerCall } from "./fhe-protocol";
    
    /** A per-unlock FHE session. Terminate it on wallet lock or account change. */
    export class FheController {
      readonly #worker: Worker;
      readonly #transport: ReturnType<typeof createFheTransport>;
      readonly #pending = new Map<number, { resolve: (value: unknown) => void; reject: (error: Error) => void }>();
      #sequence = 0;
      #closed = false;
    
      private constructor(account: string, chainId: number) {
        this.#transport = createFheTransport(anon, account, chainId);
        this.#worker = new Worker(new URL("./fhe-worker.ts", import.meta.url), { type: "module", name: "private-swap-fhe" });
        this.#worker.onmessage = (event: MessageEvent<FromFheWorker>) => void this.#onMessage(event.data);
        this.#worker.onerror = () => this.close();
        this.#worker.onmessageerror = () => this.close();
      }
    
      static async open(account: string, chainId: number): Promise<FheController> {
        const controller = new FheController(account, chainId);
        try {
          await controller.#call({ op: "init", account, chainId });
          return controller;
        } catch (error) {
          controller.close();
          throw error;
        }
      }
    
      async encrypt(contract: Hex, amount: bigint, direction?: 1 | 2): Promise<EncryptedInput> {
        return await this.#call({ op: "encrypt", contract, amount: amount.toString(), ...(direction ? { direction } : {}) }) as EncryptedInput;
      }
    
      close(): void {
        if (this.#closed) return;
        this.#closed = true;
        this.#worker.terminate();
        for (const request of this.#pending.values()) request.reject(new Error("FHE session closed"));
        this.#pending.clear();
      }
    
      #post(message: ToFheWorker, transfer: Transferable[] = []): void {
        if (!this.#closed) this.#worker.postMessage(message, transfer);
      }
    
      #call(call: WorkerCall): Promise<unknown> {
        if (this.#closed) return Promise.reject(new Error("FHE session closed"));
        const id = ++this.#sequence;
        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
          this.#pending.set(id, { resolve, reject });
          this.#post({ type: "call", id, call });
        });
      }
    
      async #onMessage(message: FromFheWorker): Promise<void> {
        if (this.#closed) return;
        if (message.type === "result") {
          const request = this.#pending.get(message.id);
          if (!request) return;
          this.#pending.delete(message.id);
          if (message.ok) request.resolve(message.value);
          else request.reject(new Error(message.error));
          return;
        }
        if (message.type === "rpc") {
          try {
            const value = await this.#transport.rpc(message.method, message.params);
            this.#post({ type: "rpc-result", id: message.id, ok: true, value });
          } catch (error) {
            this.#post({ type: "rpc-result", id: message.id, ok: false, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) });
          }
          return;
        }
        try {
          const response = await this.#transport.fetch(message.url, message.method, message.headers, message.body);
          const body = response.body ? await response.arrayBuffer() : null;
          const headers: [string, string][] = [];
          response.headers.forEach((value, key) => headers.push([key, value]));
          this.#post({ type: "fetch-result", id: message.id, ok: true, status: response.status, statusText: response.statusText, headers, body, url: response.url }, body ? [body] : []);
        } catch (error) {
          this.#post({ type: "fetch-result", id: message.id, ok: false, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) });
        }
      }
    }

## Assistant

I’ve added a local contract implementation that matches encrypted orders in whole lots, so the two tokens can have different unit prices without mixing their quantities. It is still a development contract: its price is fixed at deployment, and the oracle, per-order protections and recovery paths remain to be implemented before any live use.

        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Orders — encrypted direction and amount, one shared entry point
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    
        /// @notice Submit an order from the funded confidential balance.
        /// @param encAmount encrypted order size in c-token units (6 dp).
        /// @param encDirection encrypted direction: 1 = sell A→B, 2 = sell B→A.
        /// Both values come from ONE client-side encrypted input; the same input
        /// proof covers both handles. Neither value is ever public.
        function submitOrder(externalEuint64 encAmount, bytes calldata amountProof, externalEuint8 encDirection, bytes calldata directionProof) external {
            RoundState storage round = rounds[currentRound];
            require(!round.closed, RoundNotOpen());
            require(euint8.unwrap(_orderDir[currentRound][msg.sender]) == 0, OrderAlreadySubmitted());
            require(_orderers[currentRound].length < MAX_ORDERS_PER_ROUND, TooManyOrders());
    
            euint64 amt = FHE.fromExternal(encAmount, amountProof);
            euint8 dir = FHE.fromExternal(encDirection, directionProof);
    
            // Debit the funded balance of the chosen asset. The direction stays
            // encrypted: each side's debit is the amount masked by the direction
            // bit, and an underfunded order is silently zeroed (OZ pattern) so
            // the revert-or-not channel never leaks the balance comparison.
            ebool isA = FHE.eq(dir, DIR_A_TO_B);
            ebool isB = FHE.eq(dir, DIR_B_TO_A);
            // Divide by each public lot size, then require an exact multiple.
            // Invalid, excessive, or underfunded orders are silently zeroed so
            // those checks do not create a public balance/direction oracle.
            euint64 lotsA = FHE.div(amt, lotA);
            euint64 lotsB = FHE.div(amt, lotB);
            ebool validA = FHE.and(FHE.eq(FHE.mul(lotsA, lotA), amt), FHE.le(lotsA, maxLotsPerOrder));
            ebool validB = FHE.and(FHE.eq(FHE.mul(lotsB, lotB), amt), FHE.le(lotsB, maxLotsPerOrder));
            euint64 debitA = FHE.select(FHE.and(isA, validA), amt, FHE.asEuint64(0));
            euint64 debitB = FHE.select(FHE.and(isB, validB), amt, FHE.asEuint64(0));
    
            euint64 balA = FHE.isInitialized(_funded[msg.sender][0]) ? _funded[msg.sender][0] : FHE.asEuint64(0);
            euint64 balB = FHE.isInitialized(_funded[msg.sender][1]) ? _funded[msg.sender][1] : FHE.asEuint64(0);
            debitA = FHE.select(FHE.ge(balA, debitA), debitA, FHE.asEuint64(0));
            debitB = FHE.select(FHE.ge(balB, debitB), debitB, FHE.asEuint64(0));
    
            euint64 newBalA = FHE.sub(balA, debitA);
            euint64 newBalB = FHE.sub(balB, debitB);
            FHE.allowThis(newBalA);
            FHE.allowThis(newBalB);
            FHE.allow(newBalA, msg.sender);
            FHE.allow(newBalB, msg.sender);
            _funded[msg.sender][0] = newBalA;
            _funded[msg.sender][1] = newBalB;
    
            // Round totals (encrypted forever).
            euint64 prevA = FHE.isInitialized(round.aToB) ? round.aToB : FHE.asEuint64(0);
            euint64 prevB = FHE.isInitialized(round.bToA) ? round.bToA : FHE.asEuint64(0);
            euint64 orderLots = FHE.add(FHE.div(debitA, lotA), FHE.div(debitB, lotB));
            round.aToB = FHE.add(prevA, FHE.div(debitA, lotA));
            round.bToA = FHE.add(prevB, FHE.div(debitB, lotB));
            FHE.allowThis(round.aToB);
            FHE.allowThis(round.bToA);
    
            _orderAmount[currentRound][msg.sender] = orderLots;
            _orderDir[currentRound][msg.sender] = dir;
            FHE.allowThis(_orderAmount[currentRound][msg.sender]);
            FHE.allowThis(dir);
    
            if (!_isOrderer[currentRound][msg.sender]) {
                _orderers[currentRound].push(msg.sender);
                _isOrderer[currentRound][msg.sender] = true;
                ++distinctSubmitters;
            }
    
            emit OrderSubmitted(currentRound, msg.sender);
        }
    
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Phase 1 — close the round: encrypted netting, nothing decrypts
        // ------------------------------------------------------------------
    
        /// @notice Close the round and run the greedy pairwise netting entirely
        /// inside FHE. Per-order matched/residual amounts are computed and stored
        /// encrypted. Only the NET residual and the surplus SIDE become publicly
        /// decryptable — never the two totals, never the matched amount, never
        /// any user's order.
        function closeRound() external {
            require(block.timestamp >= lastUpdateTime + minRoundDuration, TooEarlyForNextRound());
            require(distinctSubmitters >= minDistinctSubmitters, NotEnoughSubmitters());
            RoundState storage round = rounds[currentRound];
            require(!round.closed, RoundAlreadyClosed());
            round.closed = true;
    
            address[] memory orderers = _orderers[currentRound];
            uint256 n = orderers.length;
    
            // Greedy pairwise netting: for every opposite-direction pair, match

## Assistant

The local FHE tests now cover non-1:1 matching, whole-lot validation and large payouts, and settlement proof preparation no longer depends on one designated caller. I’m adding recovery for rounds that time out, including the case where the residual has already entered the wrapper’s asynchronous unwrap process.

    +    /// @notice After a timeout, make an unexecuted round refundable. If a
    +    /// residual was already burned for asynchronous unwrap, its gateway proof
    +    /// must first finalize the unwrap; re-wrapping restores the vault backing.
    +    /// A stalled gateway can still delay this path until a proof is available.
    +    function expireRound(uint256 roundId, uint64 unwrapCleartext, bytes calldata proof) external {
    +        RoundState storage round = rounds[roundId];
    +        require(!round.executed && !refundable[roundId], RoundAlreadySettled());
    +        if (!round.closed) {
    +            require(roundId == currentRound, RoundNotOpen());
    +            require(block.timestamp >= lastUpdateTime + refundDelay, RefundTooEarly());
    +            round.closed = true;
    +            closedAt[roundId] = block.timestamp;
    +            ++currentRound;
    +            distinctSubmitters = 0;
    +            lastUpdateTime = block.timestamp;
    +        } else {
    +            require(block.timestamp >= closedAt[roundId] + refundDelay, RefundTooEarly());
    +        }
    +        if (round.prepared && round.netSwapped > 0) {
    +            ERC7984ERC20Wrapper wrapper = round.surplusSide == DIR_A_TO_B ? cTokenA : cTokenB;
    +            uint64 expected = round.netSwapped * (round.surplusSide == DIR_A_TO_B ? lotA : lotB);
    +            if (unwrapCleartext != expected) revert UnwrapMismatch(unwrapCleartext, expected);
    +            wrapper.finalizeUnwrap(euint64.wrap(round.unwrapHandle), unwrapCleartext, proof);
    +            IERC20 underlying = IERC20(wrapper.underlying());
    +            uint256 underlyingAmount = uint256(expected) * wrapper.rate();
    +            underlying.forceApprove(address(wrapper), underlyingAmount);
    +            wrapper.wrap(address(this), underlyingAmount);
    +        }
    +        refundable[roundId] = true;
    +        emit RoundRefundable(roundId);
    +    }
    +
    +    /// @notice Restore one order's original encrypted input after expiry.
    +    /// Permissionless; the refund is credited only to the original owner.
    +    function refundOrder(uint256 roundId, address user) external {
    +        require(refundable[roundId], RoundNotRefundable());
    +        require(!orderRefunded[roundId][user], AlreadyRefunded());
    +        euint8 dir = _orderDir[roundId][user];
    +        require(euint8.unwrap(dir) != 0, NoOrder());
    +        orderRefunded[roundId][user] = true;
    +        euint64 lots = _orderAmount[roundId][user];
    +        euint64 inA = FHE.select(FHE.eq(dir, DIR_A_TO_B), FHE.mul(lots, lotA), FHE.asEuint64(0));
    +        euint64 inB = FHE.select(FHE.eq(dir, DIR_B_TO_A), FHE.mul(lots, lotB), FHE.asEuint64(0));
    +        euint64 balA = FHE.isInitialized(_funded[user][0]) ? _funded[user][0] : FHE.asEuint64(0);
    +        euint64 balB = FHE.isInitialized(_funded[user][1]) ? _funded[user][1] : FHE.asEuint64(0);
    +        euint64 newA = FHE.add(balA, inA);
    +        euint64 newB = FHE.add(balB, inB);
    +        FHE.allowThis(newA);
    +        FHE.allowThis(newB);
    +        FHE.allow(newA, user);
    +        FHE.allow(newB, user);
    +        _funded[user][0] = newA;
    +        _funded[user][1] = newB;
    +        emit OrderRefunded(roundId, user);
    +    }
    +
         function _payout(uint256 roundId, address user, euint8 dir, ebool isA) internal returns (euint64 payout) {

    +    const days = 1;
    +    decryptSession = { keypair, contracts, start, days };
    +    return instance.createEIP712(keypair.publicKey, contracts, start, days);
    +  }
    +  if (call.op === "decrypt") {
    +    const session = decryptSession;
    +    if (!session || Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) >= session.start + session.days * 86400) throw new Error("Decryption authorization is missing or expired");
    +    if (!call.handles.length || call.handles.length > 3) throw new Error("Invalid decryption handle list");
    +    const handles = call.handles.map(({ handle, contractAddress }) => {
    +      const contract = getAddress(contractAddress);
    +      if (!session.contracts.includes(contract) || !/^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$/.test(handle)) throw new Error("Invalid decryption target");
    +      return { handle, contractAddress: contract };
    +    });
    +    return instance.userDecrypt(handles, session.keypair.privateKey, session.keypair.publicKey, call.signature, session.contracts, owner, session.start, session.days);
    +  }
       const amount = BigInt(call.amount);

## Assistant

The development contract now supports encrypted minimum outputs and timed refunds. The local tests exercise recovery both before settlement and after an asynchronous unwrap has been prepared; a gateway proof is still required to recover the latter. I’m moving the pricing input from a fixed constructor value toward a per-round oracle snapshot, which is necessary for a WETH/token market.

    // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
    pragma solidity ^0.8.27;
    
    import {ILotPriceOracle} from "../interfaces/ILotPriceOracle.sol";
    
    contract MockLotPriceOracle is ILotPriceOracle {
        uint64 public a;
        uint64 public b;
        uint256 public updatedAt;
    
        constructor(uint64 lotA, uint64 lotB) { setPrice(lotA, lotB, block.timestamp); }
        function setPrice(uint64 lotA, uint64 lotB, uint256 timestamp) public { a = lotA; b = lotB; updatedAt = timestamp; }
        function latestLotPrice() external view returns (uint64, uint64, uint256) { return (a, b, updatedAt); }
    }

    Add: /home/sahilkaushik/study_material/blockchain/prawl/private-swap/contracts/ChainlinkLotPriceOracle.sol
    // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
    pragma solidity ^0.8.27;
    
    import {Math} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol";
    import {IERC20Metadata} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/extensions/IERC20Metadata.sol";
    import {ILotPriceOracle} from "./interfaces/ILotPriceOracle.sol";
    
    interface IAggregatorV3 {
        function decimals() external view returns (uint8);
        function latestRoundData() external view returns (uint80 roundId, int256 answer, uint256 startedAt, uint256 updatedAt, uint80 answeredInRound);
    }
    
    /// @notice Crosses two independent USD feeds into a token A:B lot price.
    /// @dev Both underlyings must have 18 decimals and their ERC-7984 wrappers
    ///      must use six decimals. Deployment must verify feed/asset mappings,
    ///      feed independence, liquidity and heartbeat. This is unaudited.
    contract ChainlinkLotPriceOracle is ILotPriceOracle {
        uint64 public constant A_LOT = 1_000_000; // one token at six decimals
        uint256 private constant MAX_NORMALIZED_PRICE = 1e30;
        IAggregatorV3 public immutable feedA;
        IAggregatorV3 public immutable feedB;
        uint8 public immutable decimalsA;
        uint8 public immutable decimalsB;
        uint16 public immutable maxRoundingBps;
    
        error InvalidFeed();
        error InsufficientPrecision();
    
        constructor(address tokenA, address tokenB, address feedA_, address feedB_, uint16 maxRoundingBps_) {
            require(tokenA != address(0) && tokenB != address(0) && tokenA != tokenB, "invalid tokens");
            require(feedA_ != address(0) && feedB_ != address(0) && feedA_ != feedB_, "invalid feeds");
            require(IERC20Metadata(tokenA).decimals() == 18 && IERC20Metadata(tokenB).decimals() == 18, "unsupported token decimals");
            require(maxRoundingBps_ <= 100, "rounding bound too high");
            feedA = IAggregatorV3(feedA_);
            feedB = IAggregatorV3(feedB_);
            decimalsA = IAggregatorV3(feedA_).decimals();
            decimalsB = IAggregatorV3(feedB_).decimals();
            require(decimalsA <= 36 && decimalsB <= 36, "unsupported feed decimals");
            maxRoundingBps = maxRoundingBps_;
        }
    
        function latestLotPrice() external view returns (uint64 lotA, uint64 lotB, uint256 updatedAt) {
            (uint256 a, uint256 atA) = _read(feedA, decimalsA);
            (uint256 b, uint256 atB) = _read(feedB, decimalsB);
            uint256 numerator = a * A_LOT;
            uint256 output = numerator / b;
            if (output == 0 || output > type(uint64).max) revert InsufficientPrecision();
            // Exact inequality avoids accepting rounding above the configured
            // bound because of integer division truncation.
            if ((numerator % b) * 10_000 > numerator * maxRoundingBps) revert InsufficientPrecision();
            return (A_LOT, uint64(output), atA < atB ? atA : atB);
        }
    
        function _read(IAggregatorV3 feed, uint8 decimals_) private view returns (uint256 price, uint256 updatedAt) {
            (uint80 roundId, int256 answer,, uint256 at, uint80 answeredInRound) = feed.latestRoundData();
            if (roundId == 0 || answer <= 0 || at == 0 || at > block.timestamp || answeredInRound < roundId) revert InvalidFeed();
            price = Math.mulDiv(uint256(answer), 1e18, 10 ** uint256(decimals_));
            if (price == 0 || price > MAX_NORMALIZED_PRICE) revert InvalidFeed();
            return (price, at);
        }
    }

    // SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
    pragma solidity ^0.8.27;
    
    contract MockAggregator {
        uint8 public immutable decimals;
        uint80 public roundId = 1;
        int256 public answer;
        uint256 public updatedAt;
        uint80 public answeredInRound = 1;
        constructor(uint8 decimals_, int256 answer_) { decimals = decimals_; answer = answer_; updatedAt = block.timestamp; }
        function set(uint80 round_, int256 answer_, uint256 at_, uint80 answered_) external {
            roundId = round_; answer = answer_; updatedAt = at_; answeredInRound = answered_;
        }
        function latestRoundData() external view returns (uint80, int256, uint256, uint256, uint80) {
            return (roundId, answer, updatedAt, updatedAt, answeredInRound);
        }
    }

    import { expect } from "chai";
    import { ethers } from "hardhat";
    
    describe("ChainlinkLotPriceOracle", function () {
      async function setup() {
        const tokenA = await (await ethers.getContractFactory("ERC20Mock")).deploy("WETH", "WETH", 18);
        const tokenB = await (await ethers.getContractFactory("ERC20Mock")).deploy("Custom", "CUSTOM", 18);
        const feedA = await (await ethers.getContractFactory("MockAggregator")).deploy(8, 2000n * 10n ** 8n);
        const feedB = await (await ethers.getContractFactory("MockAggregator")).deploy(18, 2n * 10n ** 18n);
        const oracle = await (await ethers.getContractFactory("ChainlinkLotPriceOracle")).deploy(
          await tokenA.getAddress(), await tokenB.getAddress(), await feedA.getAddress(), await feedB.getAddress(), 10,
        );
        return { tokenA, tokenB, feedA, feedB, oracle };
      }
    
      it("normalizes different feed decimals and uses the older observation timestamp", async function () {
        const { feedA, feedB, oracle } = await setup();
        const now = (await ethers.provider.getBlock("latest"))!.timestamp;
        await (await feedA.set(2, 2000n * 10n ** 8n, now - 10, 2)).wait();
        await (await feedB.set(2, 2n * 10n ** 18n, now - 5, 2)).wait();
        const [a, b, updated] = await oracle.latestLotPrice();
        expect(a).to.equal(1_000_000n);
        expect(b).to.equal(1_000_000_000n);
        expect(updated).to.equal(BigInt(now - 10));
      });
    
      it("rejects nonpositive, incomplete, and future feed observations", async function () {
        const { feedA, oracle } = await setup();
        const now = (await ethers.provider.getBlock("latest"))!.timestamp;
        for (const [round, answer, at, answered] of [[1, 0, now, 1], [2, 1, now, 1], [1, 1, now + 1000, 1]]) {
          await (await feedA.set(round, answer, at, answered)).wait();
          await expect(oracle.latestLotPrice()).to.be.revertedWithCustomError(oracle, "InvalidFeed");
        }
      });
    
      it("rejects prices that cannot be represented within the rounding bound", async function () {
        const { feedA, feedB, oracle } = await setup();
        const now = (await ethers.provider.getBlock("latest"))!.timestamp;
        await (await feedA.set(1, 1, now, 1)).wait();
        await (await feedB.set(1, 10n ** 30n, now, 1)).wait();
        await expect(oracle.latestLotPrice()).to.be.revertedWithCustomError(oracle, "InsufficientPrecision");
      });
    });

## Assistant

The contract now snapshots a price for each round and rejects stale quotes; users can recover their input if the quote expires before settlement. I’ve also added a cross-feed price adapter, but a real deployment still needs a suitable independent feed for the custom token and a review of that feed’s reliability.

    import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
    import type { FromFheWorker, ToFheWorker } from "../src/swap/fhe-protocol";
    
    const account = "0x1111111111111111111111111111111111111111";
    const contract = "0x2222222222222222222222222222222222222222" as const;
    const identity = { key: "isolated-account" };
    const read = vi.fn(async () => "0x1");
    const http = vi.fn(async (_id: unknown, req: { url: string }) => ({
      status: 200, statusText: "OK", headers: new Headers(), body: new TextEncoder().encode("ok"), url: req.url,
    }));
    vi.mock("../src/lib/anon", () => ({ anon: { identities: { account: () => identity }, read, http } }));
    
    class FakeWorker {
      static last: FakeWorker;
      onmessage?: (event: MessageEvent<FromFheWorker>) => void;
      onerror?: () => void;
      onmessageerror?: () => void;
      sent: ToFheWorker[] = [];
      terminated = false;
      constructor() { FakeWorker.last = this; }
      postMessage(message: ToFheWorker) {
        this.sent.push(message);
        if (message.type === "call" && message.call.op === "init") {
          queueMicrotask(() => this.emit({ type: "result", id: message.id, ok: true, value: true }));
        }
      }
      emit(message: FromFheWorker) { this.onmessage?.({ data: message } as MessageEvent<FromFheWorker>); }
      terminate() { this.terminated = true; }
    }
    
    afterEach(() => { vi.unstubAllGlobals(); vi.clearAllMocks(); });
    
    describe("FHE controller", () => {
      it("keeps encryption in its worker and rejects pending work when closed", async () => {
        vi.stubGlobal("Worker", FakeWorker);
        const { FheController } = await import("../src/swap/fhe-controller");
        const controller = await FheController.open(account, 11155111);
        const result = controller.encrypt(contract, 42n, 1, 35n);
        const worker = FakeWorker.last;
        expect(worker.sent.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ type: "call", call: { op: "encrypt", contract, amount: "42", direction: 1, minimumOutput: "35" } });
        controller.close();
        await expect(result).rejects.toThrow("FHE session closed");
        expect(worker.terminated).toBe(true);
        await expect(controller.encrypt(contract, 1n)).rejects.toThrow("FHE session closed");
      });
    
      it("forwards worker RPC and relayer requests only through the isolated transport", async () => {
        vi.stubGlobal("Worker", FakeWorker);
        const { FheController } = await import("../src/swap/fhe-controller");
        const controller = await FheController.open(account, 11155111);
        const worker = FakeWorker.last;
        worker.emit({ type: "rpc", id: 51, method: "eth_call", params: [] });
        worker.emit({ type: "fetch", id: 52, url: "https://relayer.testnet.zama.org/v2/keyurl", method: "GET", headers: [], body: null });
        await vi.waitFor(() => expect(worker.sent).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([
          expect.objectContaining({ type: "rpc-result", id: 51, ok: true }),
          expect.objectContaining({ type: "fetch-result", id: 52, ok: true }),
        ])));
        expect(read).toHaveBeenCalledWith(identity, 11155111, "eth_call", []);
        expect(http).toHaveBeenCalledWith(identity, expect.objectContaining({ method: "GET" }));
        controller.close();
      });
    });

    import type { AnonRpcClient } from "@prawl/anon-rpc";
    import { decodeFunctionResult, encodeFunctionData, getAddress, isAddress, isHex, keccak256, parseAbi, zeroAddress, type Address, type Hex } from "viem";
    
    /** A build-time, chain-specific deployment manifest. Addresses alone are not trusted. */
    export interface SwapDeployment {
      chainId: 11155111;
      batch: Address;
      tokenA: Address;
      tokenB: Address;
      wrapperA: Address;
      wrapperB: Address;
      oracle: Address;
      router: Address;
      permit2: Address;
      pool: { currency0: Address; currency1: Address; fee: number; tickSpacing: number; hooks: Address };
      codeHashes: { batch: Hex; tokenA: Hex; tokenB: Hex; wrapperA: Hex; wrapperB: Hex; oracle: Hex; router: Hex; permit2: Hex };
    }
    
    const hashKeys = ["batch", "tokenA", "tokenB", "wrapperA", "wrapperB", "oracle", "router", "permit2"] as const;
    const batchAbi = parseAbi([
      "function VERSION() view returns (uint256)",
      "function cTokenA() view returns (address)",
      "function cTokenB() view returns (address)",
      "function priceOracle() view returns (address)",
      "function router() view returns (address)",
      "function permit2() view returns (address)",
      "function poolKey() view returns (address currency0, address currency1, uint24 fee, int24 tickSpacing, address hooks)",
    ]);
    const wrapperAbi = parseAbi(["function underlying() view returns (address)", "function rate() view returns (uint256)", "function decimals() view returns (uint8)"]);
    const tokenAbi = parseAbi(["function decimals() view returns (uint8)"]);
    
    function object(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
      if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) throw new Error("Invalid swap deployment manifest");
      return value as Record<string, unknown>;
    }
    function address(value: unknown): Address {
      if (typeof value !== "string" || !isAddress(value) || value.toLowerCase() === zeroAddress) throw new Error("Invalid swap deployment address");
      return getAddress(value);
    }
    function digest(value: unknown): Hex {
      if (typeof value !== "string" || !isHex(value) || value.length !== 66) throw new Error("Invalid swap deployment code hash");
      return value as Hex;
    }
    
    /** Parse a trusted build-time manifest and enforce the single WETH pair. */
    export function parseSwapDeployment(value: unknown, weth: string): SwapDeployment {
      const raw = object(value);
      if (raw.chainId !== 11155111) throw new Error("Swap deployment is not on Sepolia");
      const pool = object(raw.pool);
      const hashes = object(raw.codeHashes);
      const parts = Object.fromEntries(hashKeys.map((key) => [key, address(raw[key])])) as Pick<SwapDeployment, typeof hashKeys[number]>;
      if (new Set(Object.values(parts).map((a) => a.toLowerCase())).size !== hashKeys.length) throw new Error("Swap deployment addresses must be distinct");
      if (![parts.tokenA.toLowerCase(), parts.tokenB.toLowerCase()].includes(address(weth).toLowerCase())) throw new Error("Swap pool does not use this wallet's WETH");
      const currency0 = address(pool.currency0);
      const currency1 = address(pool.currency1);
      if (BigInt(currency0) >= BigInt(currency1)) throw new Error("Swap pool currencies are not sorted");
      if (![parts.tokenA.toLowerCase(), parts.tokenB.toLowerCase()].includes(currency0.toLowerCase()) ||
          ![parts.tokenA.toLowerCase(), parts.tokenB.toLowerCase()].includes(currency1.toLowerCase())) throw new Error("Swap pool assets do not match the manifest");
      if (!Number.isInteger(pool.fee) || Number(pool.fee) < 0 || Number(pool.fee) >= 1_000_000) throw new Error("Invalid swap pool fee");
      if (!Number.isInteger(pool.tickSpacing) || Number(pool.tickSpacing) < 1 || Number(pool.tickSpacing) > 32767) throw new Error("Invalid swap pool tick spacing");
      if (typeof pool.hooks !== "string" || pool.hooks.toLowerCase() !== zeroAddress) throw new Error("Swap pool hooks are not supported");
      return {
        chainId: 11155111,
        ...parts,
        pool: { currency0, currency1, fee: Number(pool.fee), tickSpacing: Number(pool.tickSpacing), hooks: zeroAddress },
        codeHashes: Object.fromEntries(hashKeys.map((key) => [key, digest(hashes[key])])) as SwapDeployment["codeHashes"],
      };
    }
    
    /** Validate on-chain code and wiring before showing transaction controls. */
    export async function verifySwapDeployment(client: AnonRpcClient, config: SwapDeployment): Promise<void> {
      const id = client.identities.publicScope("swap-deployment");
      const read = (method: string, params: unknown[]) => client.read(id, config.chainId, method, params);
      await Promise.all(hashKeys.map(async (key) => {
        const code = await read("eth_getCode", [config[key], "latest"]);
        if (typeof code !== "string" || !isHex(code) || code === "0x" || keccak256(code).toLowerCase() !== config.codeHashes[key].toLowerCase()) {
          throw new Error(`Swap deployment code mismatch: ${key}`);
        }
      }));
      async function call(to: Address, data: Hex): Promise<Hex> {
        const result = await read("eth_call", [{ to, data }, "latest"]);
        if (typeof result !== "string" || !isHex(result)) throw new Error("Invalid swap contract response");
        return result;
      }
      const batchRead = async (name: "VERSION" | "cTokenA" | "cTokenB" | "priceOracle" | "router" | "permit2" | "poolKey") =>
        decodeFunctionResult({ abi: batchAbi, functionName: name, data: await call(config.batch, encodeFunctionData({ abi: batchAbi, functionName: name })) });
      const [version, wrapperA, wrapperB, oracle, router, permit2, key] = await Promise.all([
        batchRead("VERSION"), batchRead("cTokenA"), batchRead("cTokenB"), batchRead("priceOracle"), batchRead("router"), batchRead("permit2"), batchRead("poolKey"),
      ]);
      if (version !== 4n || String(wrapperA).toLowerCase() !== config.wrapperA.toLowerCase() || String(wrapperB).toLowerCase() !== config.wrapperB.toLowerCase() ||
          String(oracle).toLowerCase() !== config.oracle.toLowerCase() || String(router).toLowerCase() !== config.router.toLowerCase() || String(permit2).toLowerCase() !== config.permit2.toLowerCase()) {
        throw new Error("Swap contract wiring mismatch");
      }
      if (!Array.isArray(key) || String(key[0]).toLowerCase() !== config.pool.currency0.toLowerCase() || String(key[1]).toLowerCase() !== config.pool.currency1.toLowerCase() ||
          Number(key[2]) !== config.pool.fee || Number(key[3]) !== config.pool.tickSpacing || String(key[4]).toLowerCase() !== zeroAddress) throw new Error("Swap contract pool mismatch");
      for (const [wrapper, token] of [[config.wrapperA, config.tokenA], [config.wrapperB, config.tokenB]] as const) {
        const underlying = decodeFunctionResult({ abi: wrapperAbi, functionName: "underlying", data: await call(wrapper, encodeFunctionData({ abi: wrapperAbi, functionName: "underlying" })) });
        const rate = decodeFunctionResult({ abi: wrapperAbi, functionName: "rate", data: await call(wrapper, encodeFunctionData({ abi: wrapperAbi, functionName: "rate" })) });
        const wrappedDecimals = decodeFunctionResult({ abi: wrapperAbi, functionName: "decimals", data: await call(wrapper, encodeFunctionData({ abi: wrapperAbi, functionName: "decimals" })) });
        const tokenDecimals = decodeFunctionResult({ abi: tokenAbi, functionName: "decimals", data: await call(token, encodeFunctionData({ abi: tokenAbi, functionName: "decimals" })) });
        if (underlying.toLowerCase() !== token.toLowerCase() || rate !== 10n ** 12n || wrappedDecimals !== 6 || tokenDecimals !== 18) throw new Error("Swap wrapper or token mismatch");
      }
    }

    import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
    import { encodeFunctionResult, keccak256, parseAbi, toFunctionSelector, zeroAddress, type Hex } from "viem";
    import type { AnonRpcClient } from "@prawl/anon-rpc";
    import { parseSwapDeployment, verifySwapDeployment, type SwapDeployment } from "../src/swap/deployment";
    
    const names = ["batch", "tokenA", "tokenB", "wrapperA", "wrapperB", "oracle", "router", "permit2"] as const;
    const addresses = Object.fromEntries(names.map((name, i) => [name, `0x${(i + 1).toString(16).padStart(40, "0")}`])) as Record<typeof names[number], Hex>;
    const codes = Object.fromEntries(names.map((name, i) => [name, `0x60${(i + 1).toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`])) as Record<typeof names[number], Hex>;
    const fixture = {
      chainId: 11155111, ...addresses,
      pool: { currency0: addresses.tokenA, currency1: addresses.tokenB, fee: 3000, tickSpacing: 60, hooks: zeroAddress },
      codeHashes: Object.fromEntries(names.map((name) => [name, keccak256(codes[name])])),
    };
    const batchAbi = parseAbi([
      "function VERSION() view returns (uint256)", "function cTokenA() view returns (address)", "function cTokenB() view returns (address)",
      "function priceOracle() view returns (address)", "function router() view returns (address)", "function permit2() view returns (address)",
      "function poolKey() view returns (address,address,uint24,int24,address)",
    ]);
    const wrapperAbi = parseAbi(["function underlying() view returns (address)", "function rate() view returns (uint256)", "function decimals() view returns (uint8)"]);
    
    function fakeClient(config: SwapDeployment, badCode = false, badWrapper = false): AnonRpcClient {
      return {
        identities: { publicScope: () => ({ key: "public-swap-config" }) },
        read: async (_id: unknown, chainId: number, method: string, params: unknown[]) => {
          expect(chainId).toBe(11155111);
          if (method === "eth_getCode") {
            const index = names.findIndex((name) => config[name].toLowerCase() === String(params[0]).toLowerCase());
            return badCode && index === 0 ? "0x6000" : codes[names[index]!];
          }
          const { to, data } = params[0] as { to: string; data: string };
          const selector = data.slice(0, 10);
          if (to.toLowerCase() === config.batch.toLowerCase()) {
            if (selector === toFunctionSelector("VERSION()")) return encodeFunctionResult({ abi: batchAbi, functionName: "VERSION", result: 4n });
            const fields = { cTokenA: config.wrapperA, cTokenB: config.wrapperB, priceOracle: config.oracle, router: config.router, permit2: config.permit2 } as const;
            for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(fields)) if (selector === toFunctionSelector(`${name}()`)) {
              return encodeFunctionResult({ abi: parseAbi([`function ${name}() view returns (address)`]), functionName: name, result: value });
            }
            if (selector === toFunctionSelector("poolKey()")) return encodeFunctionResult({ abi: batchAbi, functionName: "poolKey", result: [config.pool.currency0, config.pool.currency1, 3000, 60, zeroAddress] });
          }
          if (selector === toFunctionSelector("underlying()")) return encodeFunctionResult({ abi: wrapperAbi, functionName: "underlying", result: badWrapper ? config.tokenB : to.toLowerCase() === config.wrapperA.toLowerCase() ? config.tokenA : config.tokenB });
          if (selector === toFunctionSelector("rate()")) return encodeFunctionResult({ abi: wrapperAbi, functionName: "rate", result: 10n ** 12n });
          if (selector === toFunctionSelector("decimals()")) return encodeFunctionResult({ abi: wrapperAbi, functionName: "decimals", result: [config.wrapperA, config.wrapperB].some((a) => a.toLowerCase() === to.toLowerCase()) ? 6 : 18 });
          throw new Error(`unexpected read ${method}`);
        },
      } as unknown as AnonRpcClient;
    }
    
    describe("swap deployment verification", () => {
      it("accepts a pinned WETH pair only after verifying code, pool, wrappers and decimals", async () => {
        const config = parseSwapDeployment(fixture, addresses.tokenA);
        await expect(verifySwapDeployment(fakeClient(config), config)).resolves.toBeUndefined();
      });
    
      it("rejects other chains, mismatched WETH and malformed pool or code hashes", () => {
        expect(() => parseSwapDeployment({ ...fixture, chainId: 1 }, addresses.tokenA)).toThrow();
        expect(() => parseSwapDeployment(fixture, addresses.oracle)).toThrow();
        expect(() => parseSwapDeployment({ ...fixture, pool: { ...fixture.pool, hooks: addresses.oracle } }, addresses.tokenA)).toThrow();
        expect(() => parseSwapDeployment({ ...fixture, codeHashes: { ...fixture.codeHashes, batch: "0x" } }, addresses.tokenA)).toThrow();
      });
    
      it("rejects changed contract code and wrapper wiring", async () => {
        const config = parseSwapDeployment(fixture, addresses.tokenA);
        await expect(verifySwapDeployment(fakeClient(config, true), config)).rejects.toThrow("code mismatch");
        await expect(verifySwapDeployment(fakeClient(config, false, true), config)).rejects.toThrow("wrapper or token mismatch");
      });
    });
