    import { useState } from "react";
    import { formatEther, isAddress, parseEther } from "viem";
    import type { Balance } from "@prawl/railgun";
    import { useWallet } from "../lib/use-wallet";
    import { maxPrivateAmountWei, privateFeeWei, privateGasEstimateWei, privateSendEth, unshieldEth } from "../lib/wallet";
    
    type Tab = "send" | "unshield";
    
    /** Spendable private ETH: WETH notes whose PPOI status is Valid. */
    function spendable(balances: Balance[] | undefined, weth?: string): bigint {
      return (balances ?? [])
        .filter((b) => b.token !== undefined && b.token === weth && b.poiStatus === "Valid")
        .reduce((sum, b) => sum + BigInt(b.amount), 0n);
    }
    
    /** `mode` fixes the form to Send or Withdraw (used inside an action sheet); without it the card has both tabs. */
    export function PrivateActions({ mode }: { mode?: Tab } = {}) {
      const w = useWallet();
      const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>(mode ?? "send");
      const [to, setTo] = useState("");
      const [amount, setAmount] = useState("");
      const [asWeth, setAsWeth] = useState(false);
      const [error, setError] = useState<string>();
      if (!w.railgunAddress) return null;
    
      const available = spendable(w.balances, w.wrappedBaseToken);
      const running = w.action && !w.action.done;
      const lastShield = w.action?.kind === "shield" && w.action.done && !w.action.error;
    
      function submit() {
        setError(undefined);
        let wei: bigint;
        try {
          wei = parseEther(amount);
        } catch {
          return setError("Enter an amount in ETH");
        }
        if (wei <= 0n) return setError("Enter an amount above zero");
        const kind = tab;
        if (wei + privateFeeWei(kind, wei) + privateGasEstimateWei() > available) {
          const max = maxPrivateAmountWei(kind, available);
          return setError(
            `${formatEther(available)} ETH is spendable, and about ${formatEther(privateGasEstimateWei())} ETH of gas comes out of it` +
              (kind === "unshield" ? ", plus the 0.25% fee" : "") +
              (max > 0n ? `. Try at most ${formatEther(max)} ETH.` : "."),
          );
        }
        if (tab === "send") {
          if (!/^0zk1[0-9a-z]+$/.test(to)) return setError("Enter a private 0zk address");
          if (to === w.railgunAddress) return setError("That is your own address");
          void privateSendEth(to, wei);
        } else {
          if (!isAddress(to)) return setError("Enter a public 0x address");
          if (w.evmAddress && to.toLowerCase() === w.evmAddress.toLowerCase()) {
            return setError("Withdrawing to the account you shielded from links the two. Use a fresh address.");
          }
          void unshieldEth(to, wei, { asWeth });
        }
      }
    
      return (
        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-5">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-sm">
            {(mode ? [] : (["send", "unshield"] as const)).map((t) => (
              <button key={t} onClick={() => setTab(t)} className={`whitespace-nowrap ${tab === t ? "font-medium text-zinc-100" : "text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-300"}`}>
                {t === "send" ? "Send privately" : "Withdraw to public"}
              </button>
            ))}
            <span className="ml-auto whitespace-nowrap font-mono text-xs text-zinc-400">spendable: {formatEther(available)} ETH</span>
          </div>
          <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-zinc-500">
            {tab === "send"
              ? "Only you and the recipient see the amount. Gas is paid from your private balance by a fresh one-time account."
              : "The recipient and amount become public, not who sent it. A 0.25% RAILGUN fee is added."}{" "}
            About {formatEther(privateGasEstimateWei())} ETH of gas.
          </p>
          <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-2">
            <input
              value={to}
              onChange={(e) => setTo(e.target.value.trim())}

## Assistant

The contract tests now cover stale-price rejection, round price snapshots, encrypted minimums and recovery. On the wallet side, I’ve added deployment verification that checks the configured WETH pair, contract bytecode hashes, wrappers and pool wiring before transaction controls can be enabled.

    /// @notice Fixed-supply 18-decimal token for the Prawl swap pair.

            require(bytes(name_).length != 0 && bytes(symbol_).length != 0, "empty metadata");

    import { expect } from "chai";
    import { ethers } from "hardhat";
    
    describe("PrawlToken", () => {
      it("mints a fixed 18-decimal supply to the configured holder", async () => {
        const [, holder, recipient] = await ethers.getSigners();
        const token = await (await ethers.getContractFactory("PrawlToken")).deploy("Prawl Token", "PRAWL", holder.address, 1000n);
        expect(await token.decimals()).to.equal(18);
        expect(await token.totalSupply()).to.equal(1000n);
        await (await token.connect(holder).transfer(recipient.address, 20n)).wait();
        expect(await token.balanceOf(recipient.address)).to.equal(20n);
        expect(await token.totalSupply()).to.equal(1000n);
      });
    
      it("rejects empty metadata and an invalid initial supply", async () => {
        const [holder] = await ethers.getSigners();
        const factory = await ethers.getContractFactory("PrawlToken");
        await expect(factory.deploy("", "PRAWL", holder.address, 1)).to.be.revertedWith("empty metadata");
        await expect(factory.deploy("Prawl", "PRAWL", ethers.ZeroAddress, 1)).to.be.revertedWith("invalid initial supply");
        await expect(factory.deploy("Prawl", "PRAWL", holder.address, 0)).to.be.revertedWith("invalid initial supply");
      });
    });

    /** A bottom sheet (full width on a phone, a centred card on desktop). Esc and the backdrop close it. */
    export function Sheet({ open, title, onClose, children }: { open: boolean; title: string; onClose: () => void; children: ReactNode }) {
      const id = useId();
      const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
      useEffect(() => {
        if (!open) return;
        const key = (e: KeyboardEvent) => e.key === "Escape" && onClose();
        window.addEventListener("keydown", key);
        ref.current?.focus();
        const prev = document.body.style.overflow;
        document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
        return () => {
          window.removeEventListener("keydown", key);
          document.body.style.overflow = prev;
        };
      }, [open, onClose]);
      if (!open) return null;
      return (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end justify-center sm:items-center">
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/70" onClick={onClose} aria-hidden="true" />
          <div
            ref={ref}
            role="dialog"
            aria-modal="true"
            aria-labelledby={id}
            tabIndex={-1}
            className="relative flex max-h-[92%] w-full max-w-[420px] flex-col rounded-t-3xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 outline-none sm:rounded-3xl"
          >

    Add: /home/sahilkaushik/study_material/blockchain/prawl/apps/wallet/src/swap/service.ts
    import type { AnonRpcClient } from "@prawl/anon-rpc";
    import type { Call } from "@prawl/railgun";
    import type { KmsUserDecryptEIP712Type } from "@zama-fhe/relayer-sdk/web";
    import { decodeFunctionResult, encodeFunctionData, getAddress, isHex, parseAbi, type Address, type Hex } from "viem";
    import type { SentTx } from "../lib/tx";
    import { verifySwapDeployment, type SwapDeployment } from "./deployment";
    import { FheController } from "./fhe-controller";
    
    const tokenAbi = parseAbi(["function balanceOf(address) view returns (uint256)", "function allowance(address,address) view returns (uint256)", "function approve(address,uint256) returns (bool)"]);
    const wrapperAbi = parseAbi([
      "function confidentialBalanceOf(address) view returns (bytes32)",
      "function wrap(address,uint256)",
      "function confidentialTransferAndCall(address,bytes32,bytes,bytes) returns (bytes32)",
    ]);
    const batchAbi = parseAbi([
      "function fundedBalance(address,uint8) view returns (bytes32)",
      "function currentRound() view returns (uint256)",
      "function distinctSubmitters() view returns (uint256)",
      "function lastUpdateTime() view returns (uint256)",
      "function minRoundDuration() view returns (uint256)",
      "function roundPrices(uint256) view returns (uint64 lotA,uint64 lotB,uint64 maxLots,uint256 validUntil)",
      "function submitOrder(bytes32,bytes,bytes32,bytes,bytes32,bytes)",
      "function exit(uint8,bytes32,bytes)",
    ]);
    const oracleAbi = parseAbi(["function latestLotPrice() view returns (uint64 lotA,uint64 lotB,uint256 updatedAt)"]);
    const EMPTY_HANDLE = `0x${"00".repeat(32)}`;
    const RATE = 10n ** 12n;
    const MAX_U64 = (1n << 64n) - 1n;
    
    export interface SwapBalances {
      public: readonly [bigint, bigint];
      wrapped: readonly [bigint, bigint];
      vault: readonly [bigint, bigint];
      round: bigint;
      submitters: bigint;
      closesAt: bigint;
      lotA: bigint;
      lotB: bigint;
      priceValidUntil: bigint | undefined;
    }
    
    export interface SwapSigner {
      /** Must verify the authorization's domain and addresses before signing. */
      signDecrypt(request: KmsUserDecryptEIP712Type, contracts: readonly Address[]): Promise<Hex>;
      send(calls: readonly Call[], onStatus?: (status: string) => void): Promise<SentTx[]>;
    }
    
    /** An unlocked-wallet session. All calls and gateway I/O use Anon-RPC. */
    export class SwapService {
      readonly #client: AnonRpcClient;
      readonly #account: Address;
      readonly #signer: SwapSigner;
      readonly #fhe: FheController;
      readonly config: SwapDeployment;
      #closed = false;
      #running = false;
      #decryptSignature: Hex | undefined;
    
      private constructor(client: AnonRpcClient, account: Address, config: SwapDeployment, signer: SwapSigner, fhe: FheController) {
        this.#client = client; this.#account = account; this.config = config; this.#signer = signer; this.#fhe = fhe;
      }
    
      static async open(client: AnonRpcClient, account: Address, config: SwapDeployment, signer: SwapSigner): Promise<SwapService> {
        await verifySwapDeployment(client, config);
        const fhe = await FheController.open(getAddress(account), config.chainId);
        return new SwapService(client, getAddress(account), config, signer, fhe);
      }
    
      close(): void { this.#closed = true; this.#decryptSignature = undefined; this.#fhe.close(); }
      #assertOpen(): void { if (this.#closed) throw new Error("Swap session is closed"); }
    
      async #call(to: Address, data: Hex): Promise<Hex> {
        this.#assertOpen();
        const result = await this.#client.read(this.#client.identities.account(this.#account), this.config.chainId, "eth_call", [{ to, data }, "latest"]);
        if (typeof result !== "string" || !isHex(result)) throw new Error("Invalid swap contract response");
        this.#assertOpen();
        return result;
      }
    
      async #decrypt(handles: readonly { handle: Hex; contractAddress: Address }[]): Promise<bigint[]> {
        this.#assertOpen();
        const nonzero = handles.filter(({ handle }) => handle.toLowerCase() !== EMPTY_HANDLE);
        if (!nonzero.length) return handles.map(() => 0n);
        if (!this.#decryptSignature) {
          const contracts = [this.config.batch, this.config.wrapperA, this.config.wrapperB];
          const authorization = await this.#fhe.prepareDecrypt(contracts);
          this.#assertOpen();
          this.#decryptSignature = await this.#signer.signDecrypt(authorization, contracts);
          this.#assertOpen();
        }
        const values = await this.#fhe.decrypt(nonzero, this.#decryptSignature);
        this.#assertOpen();
        return handles.map(({ handle }) => {
          if (handle.toLowerCase() === EMPTY_HANDLE) return 0n;
          const value = values[handle] ?? values[handle.toLowerCase() as Hex];
          if (typeof value !== "bigint") throw new Error("Unexpected confidential balance response");
          return value;
        });
      }
    
      async balances(): Promise<SwapBalances> {
        const tokens = [this.config.tokenA, this.config.tokenB] as const;
        const wrappers = [this.config.wrapperA, this.config.wrapperB] as const;
        const publicBalances = await Promise.all(tokens.map(async (token) => decodeFunctionResult({ abi: tokenAbi, functionName: "balanceOf", data: await this.#call(token, encodeFunctionData({ abi: tokenAbi, functionName: "balanceOf", args: [this.#account] })) })));
        const handles = await Promise.all(wrappers.map(async (wrapper, i) => ({
          wrapper: decodeFunctionResult({ abi: wrapperAbi, functionName: "confidentialBalanceOf", data: await this.#call(wrapper, encodeFunctionData({ abi: wrapperAbi, functionName: "confidentialBalanceOf", args: [this.#account] })) }),
          vault: decodeFunctionResult({ abi: batchAbi, functionName: "fundedBalance", data: await this.#call(this.config.batch, encodeFunctionData({ abi: batchAbi, functionName: "fundedBalance", args: [this.#account, i] })) }),
        })));
        const [wrappedA, wrappedB, vaultA, vaultB] = await this.#decrypt([
          { handle: handles[0]!.wrapper, contractAddress: wrappers[0] }, { handle: handles[1]!.wrapper, contractAddress: wrappers[1] },
          { handle: handles[0]!.vault, contractAddress: this.config.batch }, { handle: handles[1]!.vault, contractAddress: this.config.batch },
        ]);
        const round = decodeFunctionResult({ abi: batchAbi, functionName: "currentRound", data: await this.#call(this.config.batch, encodeFunctionData({ abi: batchAbi, functionName: "currentRound" })) });
        const [submitters, lastUpdate, duration, price] = await Promise.all([
          this.#call(this.config.batch, encodeFunctionData({ abi: batchAbi, functionName: "distinctSubmitters" })).then((data) => decodeFunctionResult({ abi: batchAbi, functionName: "distinctSubmitters", data })),
          this.#call(this.config.batch, encodeFunctionData({ abi: batchAbi, functionName: "lastUpdateTime" })).then((data) => decodeFunctionResult({ abi: batchAbi, functionName: "lastUpdateTime", data })),
          this.#call(this.config.batch, encodeFunctionData({ abi: batchAbi, functionName: "minRoundDuration" })).then((data) => decodeFunctionResult({ abi: batchAbi, functionName: "minRoundDuration", data })),
          this.#call(this.config.batch, encodeFunctionData({ abi: batchAbi, functionName: "roundPrices", args: [round] })).then((data) => decodeFunctionResult({ abi: batchAbi, functionName: "roundPrices", data })),
        ]);
        let lotA = price[0], lotB = price[1];
        if (lotA === 0n) {
          const quote = decodeFunctionResult({ abi: oracleAbi, functionName: "latestLotPrice", data: await this.#call(this.config.oracle, encodeFunctionData({ abi: oracleAbi, functionName: "latestLotPrice" })) });
          lotA = quote[0]; lotB = quote[1];
        }
        return { public: [publicBalances[0]!, publicBalances[1]!], wrapped: [wrappedA!, wrappedB!], vault: [vaultA!, vaultB!], round, submitters, closesAt: lastUpdate + duration, lotA, lotB, priceValidUntil: price[3] === 0n ? undefined : price[3] };
      }
    
      async #exclusive<T>(operation: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
        this.#assertOpen();
        if (this.#running) throw new Error("A swap operation is already running");
        this.#running = true;
        try { return await operation(); } finally { this.#running = false; }
      }
    
      async #send(calls: Call[], onStatus?: (status: string) => void): Promise<SentTx[]> {
        this.#assertOpen();
        const receipts = await this.#signer.send(calls, onStatus);
        this.#assertOpen();
        if (receipts.length !== calls.length || receipts.some((receipt) => receipt.status !== "success")) throw new Error("A swap transaction reverted");
        return receipts;
      }
    
      /** Public ERC-20 to confidential wrapper; amount is six-decimal wrapper units. */
      wrap(asset: 0 | 1, amount: bigint, onStatus?: (status: string) => void): Promise<SentTx[]> {
        return this.#exclusive(async () => {
          if (amount <= 0n || amount > MAX_U64) throw new Error("Invalid amount");
          const token = asset === 0 ? this.config.tokenA : this.config.tokenB;
          const wrapper = asset === 0 ? this.config.wrapperA : this.config.wrapperB;
          const underlyingAmount = amount * RATE;
          const balance = decodeFunctionResult({ abi: tokenAbi, functionName: "balanceOf", data: await this.#call(token, encodeFunctionData({ abi: tokenAbi, functionName: "balanceOf", args: [this.#account] })) });
          if (balance < underlyingAmount) throw new Error("Not enough public tokens to wrap");
          const allowance = decodeFunctionResult({ abi: tokenAbi, functionName: "allowance", data: await this.#call(token, encodeFunctionData({ abi: tokenAbi, functionName: "allowance", args: [this.#account, wrapper] })) });
          const calls: Call[] = [];
          if (allowance < underlyingAmount) calls.push({ to: token, value: 0n, data: encodeFunctionData({ abi: tokenAbi, functionName: "approve", args: [wrapper, underlyingAmount] }) });
          calls.push({ to: wrapper, value: 0n, data: encodeFunctionData({ abi: wrapperAbi, functionName: "wrap", args: [this.#account, underlyingAmount] }) });
          return this.#send(calls, onStatus);
        });
      }
    
      /** Move existing wrapped tokens to the swap vault. */
      fund(asset: 0 | 1, amount: bigint, onStatus?: (status: string) => void): Promise<SentTx[]> {
        return this.#exclusive(async () => {
          if (amount <= 0n || amount > MAX_U64) throw new Error("Invalid amount");
          const wrapper = asset === 0 ? this.config.wrapperA : this.config.wrapperB;
          const encrypted = await this.#fhe.encrypt(wrapper, amount);
          if (encrypted.handles.length !== 1) throw new Error("Unexpected encrypted input");
          return this.#send([{ to: wrapper, value: 0n, data: encodeFunctionData({ abi: wrapperAbi, functionName: "confidentialTransferAndCall", args: [this.config.batch, encrypted.handles[0]!, encrypted.proof, "0x00"] }) }], onStatus);
        });
      }
    
      order(direction: 1 | 2, amount: bigint, minOutput: bigint, onStatus?: (status: string) => void): Promise<SentTx[]> {
        return this.#exclusive(async () => {
          if (amount <= 0n || amount > MAX_U64 || minOutput <= 0n || minOutput > MAX_U64) throw new Error("Invalid swap amount or minimum output");
          const balances = await this.balances();
          const inputLot = direction === 1 ? balances.lotA : balances.lotB;
          const outputLot = direction === 1 ? balances.lotB : balances.lotA;
          if (inputLot === 0n || outputLot === 0n || amount % inputLot !== 0n) throw new Error("Amount must be a whole number of price lots");
          if (balances.vault[direction - 1]! < amount) throw new Error("Not enough confidential vault balance");
          if (minOutput > amount / inputLot * outputLot * 9000n / 10000n) throw new Error("Minimum output exceeds the batch's guaranteed floor");
          const encrypted = await this.#fhe.encrypt(this.config.batch, amount, direction, minOutput);
          if (encrypted.handles.length !== 3) throw new Error("Unexpected encrypted order");
          return this.#send([{ to: this.config.batch, value: 0n, data: encodeFunctionData({ abi: batchAbi, functionName: "submitOrder", args: [encrypted.handles[0]!, encrypted.proof, encrypted.handles[1]!, encrypted.proof, encrypted.handles[2]!, encrypted.proof] }) }], onStatus);
        });
      }
    
      exit(asset: 0 | 1, amount: bigint, onStatus?: (status: string) => void): Promise<SentTx[]> {
        return this.#exclusive(async () => {
          if (amount <= 0n || amount > MAX_U64) throw new Error("Invalid amount");
          const encrypted = await this.#fhe.encrypt(this.config.batch, amount);
          if (encrypted.handles.length !== 1) throw new Error("Unexpected encrypted input");
          return this.#send([{ to: this.config.batch, value: 0n, data: encodeFunctionData({ abi: batchAbi, functionName: "exit", args: [asset, encrypted.handles[0]!, encrypted.proof] }) }], onStatus);
        });
      }
    }

how much more time it will take

## Assistant

For a working Sepolia end-to-end version, I estimate another **2–5 focused development days**, assuming the FHE gateway, pool liquidity and testnet funding are available. Production readiness is substantially longer—likely **several weeks**, because it also requires a reliable price feed for the custom token, adversarial testing, an independent security audit and operational infrastructure. Those are estimates, not guarantees. I’m continuing with the wallet transaction and dashboard integration now.

    import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
    import { decodeFunctionData, encodeFunctionResult, parseAbi, toFunctionSelector, type Address, type Hex } from "viem";
    import type { AnonRpcClient } from "@prawl/anon-rpc";
    import type { SwapDeployment } from "../src/swap/deployment";
    
    const mocks = vi.hoisted(() => ({
      verify: vi.fn(async () => {}),
      encrypt: vi.fn(async (_contract: string, _amount: bigint, direction?: number) => ({

    const address = (i: number) => `0x${i.toString(16).padStart(40, "0")}` as Address;
    const account = address(1), batch = address(2), tokenA = address(3), tokenB = address(4), wrapperA = address(5), wrapperB = address(6), oracle = address(7);
    const config = { chainId: 11155111, batch, tokenA, tokenB, wrapperA, wrapperB, oracle } as SwapDeployment;
    const handle = (i: number) => `0x${i.toString(16).padStart(64, "0")}` as Hex;
    const abi = parseAbi([
      "function balanceOf(address) view returns (uint256)", "function allowance(address,address) view returns (uint256)",
      "function confidentialBalanceOf(address) view returns (bytes32)", "function fundedBalance(address,uint8) view returns (bytes32)",
      "function currentRound() view returns (uint256)", "function distinctSubmitters() view returns (uint256)",
      "function lastUpdateTime() view returns (uint256)", "function minRoundDuration() view returns (uint256)", "function maxPriceAge() view returns (uint256)",
      "function roundPrices(uint256) view returns (uint64,uint64,uint64,uint256)",
      "function latestLotPrice() view returns (uint64,uint64,uint256)",
    ]);
    const callAbi = parseAbi(["function approve(address,uint256) returns (bool)", "function wrap(address,uint256)", "function submitOrder(bytes32,bytes,bytes32,bytes,bytes32,bytes)"]);
    
    function client(stale = false): AnonRpcClient {
      return {
        identities: { account: () => ({ key: "account" }) },
        read: async (_id: unknown, _chain: number, method: string, params: unknown[]) => {
          if (method === "eth_getBlockByNumber") return { timestamp: "0x3e8" };
          const { to, data } = params[0] as { to: string; data: string };
          const sel = data.slice(0, 10);
          if (sel === toFunctionSelector("balanceOf(address)")) return encodeFunctionResult({ abi, functionName: "balanceOf", result: 100n * 10n ** 12n });
          if (sel === toFunctionSelector("allowance(address,address)")) return encodeFunctionResult({ abi, functionName: "allowance", result: 0n });
          if (sel === toFunctionSelector("confidentialBalanceOf(address)")) return encodeFunctionResult({ abi, functionName: "confidentialBalanceOf", result: handle(to === wrapperA ? 1 : 2) });
          if (sel === toFunctionSelector("fundedBalance(address,uint8)")) return encodeFunctionResult({ abi, functionName: "fundedBalance", result: handle(3) });
          if (sel === toFunctionSelector("currentRound()")) return encodeFunctionResult({ abi, functionName: "currentRound", result: 1n });
          if (sel === toFunctionSelector("distinctSubmitters()")) return encodeFunctionResult({ abi, functionName: "distinctSubmitters", result: 1n });
          if (sel === toFunctionSelector("lastUpdateTime()")) return encodeFunctionResult({ abi, functionName: "lastUpdateTime", result: 950n });
          if (sel === toFunctionSelector("minRoundDuration()")) return encodeFunctionResult({ abi, functionName: "minRoundDuration", result: 60n });
          if (sel === toFunctionSelector("maxPriceAge()")) return encodeFunctionResult({ abi, functionName: "maxPriceAge", result: 1000n });
          if (sel === toFunctionSelector("roundPrices(uint256)")) return encodeFunctionResult({ abi, functionName: "roundPrices", result: [2n, 5n, 100n, stale ? 900n : 1500n] });
          throw new Error(`unexpected call ${sel}`);
        },
      } as unknown as AnonRpcClient;
    }
    
    function signer() {
      return {
        signDecrypt: vi.fn(async () => "0x1234" as Hex),
        send: vi.fn(async (calls: readonly unknown[]) => calls.map(() => ({ hash: handle(9), status: "success" as const, blockNumber: 1n, gasUsed: 1n }))),
      };
    }
    
    describe("swap service", () => {
      it("constructs exact ERC-20 approval and wrapper calls", async () => {
        const { SwapService } = await import("../src/swap/service");
        const sign = signer();
        const service = await SwapService.open(client(), account, config, sign);
        await service.wrap(0, 10n);
        const calls = sign.send.mock.calls[0]![0] as Array<{ to: Address; data: Hex }>;
        expect(calls.map((call) => call.to)).toEqual([tokenA, wrapperA]);
        expect(decodeFunctionData({ abi: callAbi, data: calls[0]!.data }).args).toEqual([wrapperA, 10n * 10n ** 12n]);
        expect(decodeFunctionData({ abi: callAbi, data: calls[1]!.data }).args).toEqual([account, 10n * 10n ** 12n]);
        service.close();
      });
    
      it("encrypts direction, amount and minimum for a valid whole-lot order", async () => {
        const { SwapService } = await import("../src/swap/service");
        const sign = signer();
        const service = await SwapService.open(client(), account, config, sign);
        await service.order(1, 10n, 22n);
        expect(mocks.encrypt).toHaveBeenCalledWith(batch, 10n, 1, 22n);
        const calls = sign.send.mock.calls[0]![0] as Array<{ to: Address; data: Hex }>;
        expect(calls[0]!.to).toBe(batch);
        expect(decodeFunctionData({ abi: callAbi, data: calls[0]!.data }).functionName).toBe("submitOrder");
        service.close();
      });
    
      it("refuses stale prices and unaffordable minimums before encrypting or sending", async () => {
        const { SwapService } = await import("../src/swap/service");
        mocks.encrypt.mockClear();
        const sign = signer();
        const service = await SwapService.open(client(true), account, config, sign);
        await expect(service.order(1, 10n, 22n)).rejects.toThrow("expired");
        expect(mocks.encrypt).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
        expect(sign.send).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
        service.close();
      });
    });

    async function guarded(fn: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {
      update({ phase: "unlocking", error: undefined });
      try {
        await fn();
      } catch (e) {
        keys = undefined;
        update({ phase: "error", error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) });
      }
    }
    
    export function createWallet(): Promise<void> {
      return guarded(async () => {
        const { root, credentialId, bornAt } = await createPasskey(undefined, { clockSkewS });
        await store.set(CREDENTIAL_KEY, credentialId);
        update({ hasPasskey: true });
        await start(root, { fresh: true, ...(bornAt !== undefined ? { bornAt } : {}) });
      });
    }
    
    /** Unlock with the remembered passkey, or (recovery on a new device) let the user pick any Prawl passkey. */
    export function unlockWallet(): Promise<void> {
      return guarded(async () => {
        const remembered = await store.get<string>(CREDENTIAL_KEY);
        const { root, credentialId, bornAt } = await unlockWithPasskey(remembered);
        if (!remembered) await store.set(CREDENTIAL_KEY, credentialId);
        update({ hasPasskey: true });
        await start(root, { fresh: false, ...(bornAt !== undefined ? { bornAt } : {}) });
      });
    }
    
    /**
     * Forget the saved checkpoint and scan all RAILGUN history again, ignoring the
     * snapshot and the passkey's creation time. The escape hatch if a balance
     * looks wrong.
     */
    export async function rescanWallet(): Promise<void> {
      epoch += 1;
      stopAutoSync();
      update({ phase: "opening", balances: undefined, poiQueue: undefined, poiError: undefined, error: undefined });
      try {
        await railgun().call("close", undefined as never);
        await openRailgun({ fresh: false, discardCheckpoint: true });
      } catch (e) {
        update({ phase: "error", error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e) });
      }
    }
    
    export async function lockWallet(): Promise<void> {
      epoch += 1;
      stopAutoSync();
      keys = undefined;
      activityLog = undefined;
      await railgun().call("close", undefined as never);
      update({ phase: "locked", railgunAddress: undefined, evmAddress: undefined, balances: undefined, syncedBlock: undefined, poiQueue: undefined, poiError: undefined, activity: undefined, action: undefined, publicWei: undefined, privateGasWei: undefined });
    }

      const [done, setDone] = useState(false);
      return (
        <button
          title="Copy transaction hash"
          className="font-mono text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-300"
          onClick={() => void navigator.clipboard.writeText(hash).then(() => (setDone(true), setTimeout(() => setDone(false), 1200)))}
        >
          {done ? "copied" : short(hash)}

    /** The list itself (also used inside the home screen). No explorer links: opening one would reveal your IP and which transaction is yours. */
    export function ActivityList({ kinds }: { kinds?: ReadonlyArray<Activity["kind"]> }) {
      const w = useWallet();
      const items = (w.activity ?? []).filter((a) => !kinds || kinds.includes(a.kind));
      if (!w.activity) return null;
      return items.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="py-6 text-center text-sm text-zinc-500">Nothing yet.</p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="divide-y divide-zinc-900">
          {items.slice(0, 40).map((a) => (
            <li key={`${a.txHash}-${a.at}`} className="flex items-center gap-3 py-3 text-xs">
              <span className={`flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-sm ${a.ok ? "bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-400" : "bg-red-500/15 text-red-400"}`}>{a.ok ? "✓" : "✗"}</span>
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <p className="text-sm text-zinc-200">{LABEL[a.kind] ?? a.kind}</p>
                <p className="truncate text-zinc-500">
                  {new Date(a.at).toLocaleString()}
                  {a.to ? <span className="font-mono"> · {short(a.to, 10, 6)}</span> : null}
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="text-right">
                <p className="font-mono text-sm text-zinc-200">{formatEther(BigInt(a.amount))} ETH</p>
                <CopyHash hash={a.txHash} />
              </div>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      );
    }
    
    /** This wallet's own history as a card. */
    export function ActivityCard() {
      const w = useWallet();
      if (!w.railgunAddress || !w.activity) return null;
      return (
        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-5">
          <h2 className="text-sm font-medium">Activity</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-500">Kept encrypted on this device only. Copy a hash to look it up; there are no explorer links.</p>
          <ActivityList />
        </section>
      );
    }

    import type { KmsUserDecryptEIP712Type } from "@zama-fhe/relayer-sdk/web";
    import { isHex, type Address } from "viem";
    
    const VERIFIER = "0x5d8bd78e2ea6bbe41f26dfe9fdaeaA349e077478".toLowerCase();
    const TYPES = {
      UserDecryptRequestVerification: [
        { name: "publicKey", type: "bytes" },
        { name: "contractAddresses", type: "address[]" },
        { name: "startTimestamp", type: "uint256" },
        { name: "durationDays", type: "uint256" },
        { name: "extraData", type: "bytes" },
      ],
    } as const;
    
    /** Reconstruct the exact allowed signature; do not sign arbitrary SDK data. */
    export function validateDecryptAuthorization(request: KmsUserDecryptEIP712Type, contracts: readonly Address[], now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)) {
      const { domain, message } = request;
      if (request.primaryType !== "UserDecryptRequestVerification" || domain.name !== "Decryption" || domain.version !== "1" ||
          domain.chainId !== 10901n || domain.verifyingContract.toLowerCase() !== VERIFIER) throw new Error("Unexpected FHE authorization domain");
      if (contracts.length !== 3 || message.contractAddresses.length !== contracts.length ||
          message.contractAddresses.some((contract, index) => contract.toLowerCase() !== contracts[index]?.toLowerCase())) throw new Error("Unexpected FHE authorization contracts");
      if (!isHex(message.publicKey) || message.publicKey.length < 66 || message.publicKey.length > 8194 || message.extraData !== "0x00") throw new Error("Invalid FHE authorization key");
      let start: bigint, duration: bigint;
      try { start = BigInt(message.startTimestamp); duration = BigInt(message.durationDays); }
      catch { throw new Error("Invalid FHE authorization lifetime"); }
      if (duration !== 1n || start < BigInt(now - 300) || start > BigInt(now + 300)) throw new Error("Invalid FHE authorization lifetime");
      return {
        domain: { name: "Decryption", version: "1", chainId: 10901, verifyingContract: domain.verifyingContract },
        primaryType: "UserDecryptRequestVerification" as const,
        types: TYPES,
        message: { publicKey: message.publicKey, contractAddresses: [...contracts], startTimestamp: start, durationDays: duration, extraData: message.extraData },
      };
    }

          <div className="space-y-2 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 p-4 text-sm">
            <div className="flex justify-between gap-3"><span className="text-zinc-400">Spendable RAILGUN WETH</span><span className="font-mono">{wallet.balances ? formatEther(spendable) : "…"}</span></div>
            <div className="flex justify-between gap-3"><span className="text-zinc-400">Swap vault balances</span><span className="text-zinc-500">Unavailable</span></div>
            <div className="flex justify-between gap-3"><span className="text-zinc-400">Pool price and fees</span><span className="text-zinc-500">Unavailable</span></div>
            <div className="flex justify-between gap-3"><span className="text-zinc-400">Round status</span><span className="text-zinc-500">Unavailable</span></div>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-400">Order amounts and directions are encrypted in the proposed design. Funding and exits are public, and the batch’s net trade and participant addresses are visible.</p>
          <button disabled className="w-full rounded-full bg-zinc-800 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-500">Swap unavailable</button>
        </div>
      );
    }

    import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
    import { formatEther, formatUnits, parseUnits } from "viem";
    import { openSwapSession, swapConfigured, unshieldEth } from "../lib/wallet";
    import { useWallet } from "../lib/use-wallet";
    import type { SwapBalances, SwapService } from "../swap/service";
    
    /** Swap balances are separate from the RAILGUN private balance. */
    export function SwapDashboard() {
      const wallet = useWallet();
      const [service, setService] = useState<SwapService>();
      const [balances, setBalances] = useState<SwapBalances>();
      const [direction, setDirection] = useState<1 | 2>(1);
      const [amount, setAmount] = useState("");
      const [minimum, setMinimum] = useState("");
      const [status, setStatus] = useState("");
      const [error, setError] = useState("");
      const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
      const [opening, setOpening] = useState(false);
      const weth = (wallet.balances ?? []).filter((balance) => balance.token === wallet.wrappedBaseToken);
      const spendable = weth.filter((balance) => balance.poiStatus === "Valid").reduce((sum, balance) => sum + BigInt(balance.amount), 0n);
    
      useEffect(() => { setService(undefined); setBalances(undefined); }, [wallet.evmAddress, wallet.phase === "locked"]);
    
      async function connect() {
        setOpening(true); setError(""); setStatus("Checking deployment and connecting the confidential swap…");
        try {
          const session = await openSwapSession();
          const next = await session.balances();
          setService(session); setBalances(next); setStatus("");
          if (session.config.tokenB.toLowerCase() === wallet.wrappedBaseToken?.toLowerCase()) setDirection(2);
        } catch (cause) { setError(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause)); setStatus(""); }
        finally { setOpening(false); }
      }
    
      async function run(action: (session: SwapService, value: bigint) => Promise<unknown>, completed: string) {
        if (!service || busy) return;
        setError(""); setStatus("");
        let value: bigint;
        try { value = parseUnits(amount, 6); if (value <= 0n) throw new Error(); }
        catch { setError("Enter a positive amount with at most six decimal places."); return; }
        setBusy(true);
        try {
          await action(service, value);
          setStatus(completed);
          setBalances(await service.balances());
        } catch (cause) {
          setError(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause));
        } finally { setBusy(false); }
      }
    
      const inputAsset = (direction === 1 ? 0 : 1) as 0 | 1;
      const outputAsset = (1 - inputAsset) as 0 | 1;
      const labels: [string, string] = service?.config.tokenA.toLowerCase() === wallet.wrappedBaseToken?.toLowerCase() ? ["WETH", "Custom token"] : ["Custom token", "WETH"];
      let quantity = 0n;
      try { quantity = parseUnits(amount || "0", 6); } catch { /* rendered as unavailable */ }
      const lotIn = direction === 1 ? balances?.lotA : balances?.lotB;
      const lotOut = direction === 1 ? balances?.lotB : balances?.lotA;
      const estimate = lotIn && lotOut && quantity > 0n && quantity % lotIn === 0n ? quantity / lotIn * lotOut : undefined;
      const floor = estimate === undefined ? undefined : estimate * 9000n / 10000n;
      const amountText = (value: bigint) => formatUnits(value, 6);
      const isWeth = labels[inputAsset] === "WETH";
    
      return (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {!swapConfigured() ? (
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-amber-900 bg-amber-950/30 p-4 text-sm text-amber-200" role="status">
              Swaps are not available yet. A verified WETH and custom-token pool must be configured for this wallet.
            </div>
          ) : !service ? (
            <button onClick={() => void connect()} disabled={opening || busy} className="w-full rounded-full bg-emerald-500 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-950 disabled:opacity-50">
              {opening ? "Connecting…" : "Open swap dashboard"}
            </button>
          ) : null}
    
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-900/60 p-4">
            <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500">Single pool · Sepolia</p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-lg font-semibold">WETH / custom token</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-zinc-400">Orders are encrypted and settle in batches against a Uniswap v4 pool.</p>
          </div>
    
          <div className="space-y-2 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 p-4 text-sm">
            <div className="flex justify-between gap-3"><span className="text-zinc-400">Spendable RAILGUN WETH</span><span className="font-mono">{wallet.balances ? formatEther(spendable) : "…"}</span></div>
            {balances && ([0, 1] as const).map((asset) => (
              <div key={asset} className="border-t border-zinc-800 pt-2">
                <div className="font-medium text-zinc-200">{labels[asset]}</div>
                <div className="mt-1 flex justify-between text-xs text-zinc-400"><span>Public / wrapped / swap vault</span><span className="font-mono">{formatUnits(balances.public[asset], 18)} / {amountText(balances.wrapped[asset])} / {amountText(balances.vault[asset])}</span></div>
              </div>
            ))}
            {balances && <div className="border-t border-zinc-800 pt-2 text-xs text-zinc-400">Round {balances.round.toString()} · {balances.submitters.toString()} participants · earliest close {new Date(Number(balances.closesAt) * 1000).toLocaleTimeString()}</div>}
            {balances?.priceError && <p className="text-xs text-amber-400">Price unavailable: {balances.priceError}</p>}
          </div>
    
          {service && balances && (
            <div className="space-y-3 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-800 p-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm"><span>Sell {labels[inputAsset]} → {labels[outputAsset]}</span><button onClick={() => { setDirection(direction === 1 ? 2 : 1); setMinimum(""); }} disabled={busy} className="text-emerald-400">Reverse</button></div>
              <label className="block text-xs text-zinc-400">Amount
                <input type="text" inputMode="decimal" value={amount} onChange={(event) => setAmount(event.target.value)} placeholder="0.0" className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 p-3 font-mono text-sm text-zinc-100" />
              </label>
              <p className="text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-400">Indicative oracle output: {estimate === undefined ? "—" : `${amountText(estimate)} ${labels[outputAsset]}`} · guaranteed batch floor: {floor === undefined ? "—" : `${amountText(floor)} ${labels[outputAsset]}`}. Pool fee: {service.config.pool.fee / 10000}%.</p>
              <label className="block text-xs text-zinc-400">Minimum output (leave blank to use the batch floor)
                <input type="text" inputMode="decimal" value={minimum} onChange={(event) => setMinimum(event.target.value)} placeholder={floor === undefined ? "0.0" : amountText(floor)} className="mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-900 p-3 font-mono text-sm text-zinc-100" />
              </label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs">
                {isWeth && <button disabled={busy || !wallet.evmAddress} onClick={() => void run(async (_s, value) => {
                  if (!wallet.evmAddress) throw new Error("Wallet account unavailable");
                  const ok = await unshieldEth(wallet.evmAddress as `0x${string}`, value * 10n ** 12n, { asWeth: true });
                  if (!ok) throw new Error("RAILGUN withdrawal did not complete; check the wallet action status before retrying.");
                }, "WETH moved to the public account.")} className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 px-2 py-2 text-zinc-200 disabled:opacity-40">Move private WETH to public</button>}
                <button disabled={busy} onClick={() => void run((s, value) => s.wrap(inputAsset, value, setStatus), "Tokens wrapped. Fund the swap vault next.")} className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 px-2 py-2 text-zinc-200 disabled:opacity-40">Wrap public tokens</button>
                <button disabled={busy} onClick={() => void run((s, value) => s.fund(inputAsset, value, setStatus), "Confidential tokens sent to the swap vault.")} className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 px-2 py-2 text-zinc-200 disabled:opacity-40">Fund swap vault</button>
                <button disabled={busy} onClick={() => void run((s, value) => s.exit(inputAsset, value, setStatus), "Withdrawal requested. Finalization is asynchronous.")} className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-700 px-2 py-2 text-zinc-200 disabled:opacity-40">Exit vault to public</button>
              </div>
              <button disabled={busy || !!balances.priceError} onClick={() => void run((s, value) => {
                let min: bigint;
                try { min = minimum ? parseUnits(minimum, 6) : floor ?? 0n; } catch { throw new Error("Enter a valid minimum output."); }
                return s.order(direction, value, min, setStatus);
              }, "Encrypted order submitted. Track the round before placing another order.")} className="w-full rounded-full bg-emerald-500 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-950 disabled:opacity-40">Submit private swap</button>
              <button disabled={busy} onClick={() => { setBusy(true); void service.balances().then(setBalances, (cause) => setError(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause))).finally(() => setBusy(false)); }} className="w-full text-xs text-zinc-400 disabled:opacity-40">Refresh balances and round</button>
            </div>
          )}
          {status && <p role="status" className="break-words text-xs text-zinc-300">{status}</p>}
          {error && <p role="alert" className="break-words text-xs text-red-400">{error}</p>}
          <p className="text-xs leading-relaxed text-zinc-400">Order amounts, directions and minimums are encrypted. Funding and exits are public; the batch’s net trade and participant addresses are visible. Moving WETH from RAILGUN to your public account can link those activities. Swaps require public ETH for gas.</p>
        </div>
      );
    }
